39	entity "t1 : Table" as t1 order 70
40	entity "host : Host" as host order 85
41	entity "waiter : Waiter" as waiter order 90
42	entity "chef : Chef" as chef order 110
43	entity "cashier : Cashier" as cashier order 120
44	' vip (order 80) and order (order 100) are declared where they are created
45	
46	note over M, rest
47	  <b>Stage 3 | base setting (18 turns, 2 tables)</b>
48	  <b>Start:</b> turn 1, restaurant empty, 2 free tables. A VIP arrives
49	  (satisfaction 100). Burger = $15.00, 1 preparation unit.
50	  <b>Manager choice:</b> turn on Happy Hour at turn 1 or 2 (or never).
51	  <b>Outcome:</b> the VIP is PAID on turn 5, bill locked at <b>$10.80</b>,
52	  satisfaction <b>95</b>. If never activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90.
53	  <b>Host:</b> left at WAIT in turns 1-3 (the Waiter seats the VIP); in turn 4
54	  it seats customer 2, who is not traced further.
55	end note
56	
57	M -> cli : start game
58	cli -> game : startGame()
59	game -> rest : hh = getHappyHour()
60	game -> rest : menu = getMenu()
61	game -> rest : waiter = getWaiter()
62	game -> rest : chef = getChef()
63	game -> rest : cashier = getCashier()
64	game -> rest : host = getHost()
65	note right of game : GameController holds only restaurant;\nit reaches HappyHour, Menu and staff\nthrough the Restaurant getters
66	
67	loop for each turn 1..18 (turns 1-5 shown)
68	
69	  == Turn 1: VIP arrives, manager turns Happy Hour on, Waiter seats the VIP ==
70	
71	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
72	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(1)
73	  opt arrival
74	    game -> sched : vip = createArrival(1)
75	    create entity "vip : VIPCustomer" as vip order 80
76	    sched --> vip : <<create>>
77	    note right : status WAITING,\nsatisfaction 100
78	    game -> rest : registerArrival(vip)
79	    note right : vip joins\nwaitingQueue (FIFO)
80	  end
81	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant)
82	
83	  game -> game : **Start of turn** processHappyHourActivation()
84	  game -> hh : available = isAvailable()
85	  opt available (once per game)
86	    game -> cli : choice = readHappyHourChoice()
87	    cli -> M : Activate Happy Hour? (y/n)
88	    M --> cli : y
89	    alt choice == y
90	      game -> hh : activate()
91	      note right : used = true, remainingTurns = 2\n(this turn and the next)
92	    else choice == n
93	      note over game, hh : Declined now: Happy Hour stays available, so it can\nstill be switched on in turn 2 (same outcome here).\nNever activated: bill $13.50, satisfaction 90
94	    end
95	  end
96	
97	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
98	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
99	  opt active
100	    game -> hh : amount = getRecoveryAmount()
101	    loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
102	      game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(amount)
103	      note right : 100 stays 100 (capped)
104	    end
105	  end
106	
107	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
108	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
109	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
110	  M --> cli : WAIT
111	  note right of game : The Host seats one queued customer on top of the Waiter's\ntask. WAIT here: the Waiter seats the VIP instead.
112	
113	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
114	  note over game, cli : Steps 3-4 have nothing to do until a dish is READY\nor a customer is served (drawn from turn 3)
115	  note over game, cli : With several candidates the manager also picks which customer\nor order goes first; here there is only one candidate
116	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
117	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
118	  M --> cli : SEAT
119	  opt choice == SEAT
120	    game -> rest : t1 = findAvailableTable()
121	    game -> waiter : seatCustomer(vip, t1)
122	    waiter -> t1 : seat(vip)
123	    waiter -> vip : markSeated()
124	    note right : WAITING to SEATED;\nvip leaves waitingQueue
125	  end
126	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT (no order exists yet)
127	
128	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
129	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
130	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
131	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
132	    vip -> vip : adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss())
133	    note right : polymorphism: loss per waiting turn\nRegular 8, VIP 5, Critic 12\n100 to 95
134	    game -> vip : leave = shouldAbandon()
135	    opt leave (satisfaction == 0)
136	      game -> rest : handleAbandonment(vip)
137	      note right : cancel order, free table, ABANDONED
138	    end
139	  end
140	  game -> hh : endTurn()
141	  note right : remainingTurns 2 to 1
142	
143	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 2 of 3)
144	  == Turn 2: Happy Hour recovery, Waiter takes the order (price locked), Chef finishes it ==
145	
146	  game -> game : applyHappyHourRecovery()
147	  game -> hh : active = isActive()
148	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
149	    game -> vip : recoverSatisfaction(15)
150	    note right : 95 to 100 (capped at 100)
151	  end
152	
153	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
154	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
155	  game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
156	  cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
157	  M --> cli : TAKE_ORDER
158	  opt choice == TAKE_ORDER
159	    game -> waiter : order = takeOrder(vip, menu, hh, 2)
160	    waiter -> vip : item = chooseMenuItem(menu)
161	    note right : documented rule: Burger\n(1500 cents, 1 unit)
162	    waiter -> vip : bill = calculateBillCents(1500)
163	    note right : polymorphism: VIPCustomer\noverrides it, 1500 x 0.9 = 1350
164	    waiter -> hh : bill = applyDiscount(1350)
165	    note right : Happy Hour active: 1350 x 0.8 = 1080\n(declined: stays 1350)
166	    create entity "order : Order" as order order 100
167	    waiter --> order : <<create>> Order(item, 1080, 2)
168	    note right : billCents set once in the\nconstructor, no setter:\nprice locked at 1080
169	    waiter -> vip : assignOrder(order)
170	    note right : SEATED to ORDERED
171	  end
172	  game -> cli : chefChoice = readChefChoice()
173	  cli -> M : Chef: choose an order or WAIT?
174	  M --> cli : the VIP's order
175	  opt chefChoice is an order
176	    game -> chef : prepare(order)
177	    chef -> order : advancePreparation()
178	    note right : Burger 1/1 unit: PLACED to READY.\nWaiter already acted this turn:\nserved on turn 3 at the earliest
179	  end
180	
181	  game -> game : **5 Waiting and abandonment** processEndOfTurnUpdates()
182	  loop for each customer where isAwaitingService()
183	    game -> vip : reduceSatisfaction()
184	    note right : ORDERED, not yet served: 100 to 95\n(abandonment check as in turn 1)
185	  end
186	  game -> hh : endTurn()
187	  note right : remainingTurns 1 to 0\nHappy Hour is over
188	
189	  == Turn 3: Waiter serves the dish that became READY on an earlier turn ==
190	
191	  note over game, cli : Host: manager picks WAIT (no queued customer)
192	  game -> game : **2-3 Manager choices and staff actions** coordinateStaffActions()
193	  loop until the Waiter choice is accepted
194	    game -> cli : choice = readWaiterChoice()
195	    cli -> M : Waiter: SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT?
196	    M --> cli : SERVE
197	    opt choice == SERVE
198	      game -> waiter : serveOrder(vip, 3)
199	      waiter -> order : ready = isReady()
200	      alt ready (finished on turn 2)
201	        waiter -> order : markServed()
202	        note right : READY to SERVED
203	        waiter -> vip : markServed(3)
204	        note right : ORDERED to SERVED, servedTurn = 3
205	        waiter -> order : turnTaken = getTurnTaken()
206	        waiter -> vip : onServed(3 - turnTaken)
207	        note right : polymorphic hook: Critic loses 10 if served\nmore than 2 turns after ordering;\nVIP does nothing
208	      else not READY (for example, SERVE tried on turn 2)
209	        waiter --> game : rejects: "order is not READY yet"
210	        game -> cli : displayError(reason)
211	        note right : staff task is not used up,\nthe manager chooses again
212	      end
213	    end
214	  end
215	  note over game, cli : Chef: manager picks WAIT
216	  note over game : **5** served customers lose no satisfaction,\nso the waiting loop has nothing to do
217	
218	  newpage Restaurant Rush: Stage 3 sequence diagram (page 3 of 3)
219	  == Turn 4: customer 2 arrives and the Host seats them; the VIP becomes READY_TO_PAY ==
220	
221	  game -> game : **1 Arrivals** processScheduledArrival()
222	  game -> sched : arrival = isArrivalTurn(4)
223	  opt arrival
224	    game -> sched : c2 = createArrival(4)
225	    game -> rest : registerArrival(c2)
226	    note right : customer 2 joins the waitingQueue\n(created as in turn 1; type set by the schedule)
227	  end
228	  game -> cli : displayTurnStatus(restaurant)
229	
230	  game -> game : **2 Manager choices (Host)** coordinateHostAction()
231	  game -> cli : hostChoice = readHostChoice()
232	  cli -> M : Host: SEAT or WAIT?
233	  M --> cli : SEAT
234	  opt hostChoice == SEAT
235	    game -> rest : t2 = findAvailableTable()
236	    game -> host : seatCustomer(c2, t2)
237	    note right : same steps as turn 1 (Table.seat, markSeated):\nseated without using the Waiter's task
238	  end
239	  note over game, cli : Waiter and Chef: manager picks WAIT
240	
241	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
242	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
243	  opt vip is SERVED and 4 > vip.getServedTurn() (3)
244	    game -> vip : markReadyToPay(4)
245	    note right : SERVED to READY_TO_PAY, readyTurn = 4
246	  end
247	  note over game, cashier : Cashier does not charge yet:\n4 > vip.getReadyTurn() (4) is false
248	  note over game, rest : **5** customer 2 is seated and unserved, so it loses its per-turn loss;\nthe VIP (READY_TO_PAY) loses nothing
249	
250	  == Turn 5: Cashier collects the locked bill ==
251	
252	  note over game, cli : Host: WAIT. Waiter takes customer 2's order and the Chef starts it\n(not traced further: this trace follows the VIP)
253	  game -> game : **4 Eating and payment** coordinatePaymentProcessing()
254	  game -> rest : customers = getCustomers()
255	  opt oldest READY_TO_PAY customer is vip and 5 > vip.getReadyTurn() (4)
256	    game -> cashier : processPayment(vip, restaurant)
257	    cashier -> vip : order = getOrder()
258	    cashier -> order : bill = getBillCents()
259	    note right : 1080: the locked price,\nnot recalculated
260	    cashier -> rest : recordPayment(bill)
261	    note right : revenue += $10.80\nvip counts as paid with satisfaction 95
262	    cashier -> vip : markPaid()
263	    note right : READY_TO_PAY to PAID
264	    cashier -> order : markPaid()
265	    note right : SERVED to PAID
266	    cashier -> rest : releaseTableOf(vip)
267	    rest -> t1 : release()
268	  end
269	
270	  ... turns 6 to 18 run the same six steps for the other customers ...
271	end
272	
273	game -> game : **6 Result** evaluateGameResult()
274	game -> rest : paid = getPaidCustomerCount()
275	game -> rest : revenue = getRevenueCents()
276	game -> rest : avg = getAverageSatisfaction()
277	note right : won = paid >= 4 and revenue >= 4500\nand avg >= 60 (the game never ends early)
278	game -> cli : displayResult(won, restaurant)
279	alt won
280	  cli -> M : Victory
281	else not won
282	  cli -> M : Defeat
283	end
284	@enduml

{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\src\\restaurantrush\\entity\\Order.java",
  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\n/**\n * One customer's order. The status is private and moves only through the methods below, so an\n * illegal jump (for example serving a dish that is not READY) is rejected here, not in the UI.\n * Legal: PLACED -> PREPARING -> READY -> SERVED -> PAID, with PLACED -> READY for a 1-unit dish,\n * and PLACED / PREPARING / READY -> CANCELLED when the customer leaves.\n */\npublic class Order {\n    private static int nextOrderId = 1;\n\n    private final int orderId;\n    private final MenuItem item;\n    private int completedUnits;\n    private OrderStatus status;\n\n    public Order(MenuItem item) {\n        this.orderId = nextOrderId++;\n        this.item = item;\n        this.status = OrderStatus.PLACED;\n    }\n\n    /** The Chef spends one preparation unit; the last unit makes the order READY. */\n    public void advancePreparation() {\n        if (status != OrderStatus.PLACED && status != OrderStatus.PREPARING) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"The \" + item.getName() + \" order is \" + status\n                    + \" and cannot be prepared any further\");\n        }\n        completedUnits++;\n        status = completedUnits >= item.getPreparationUnits() ? OrderStatus.READY : OrderStatus.PREPARING;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isReady() {\n        return status == OrderStatus.READY;\n    }\n\n    public void markServed() {\n        require(OrderStatus.READY, \"served\");\n        status = OrderStatus.SERVED;\n    }\n\n    public void markPaid() {\n        require(OrderStatus.SERVED, \"paid\");\n        status = OrderStatus.PAID;\n    }\n\n    public void cancel() {\n        if (status != OrderStatus.PLACED && status != OrderStatus.PREPARING && status != OrderStatus.READY) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"A \" + status + \" order cannot be cancelled\");\n        }\n        status = OrderStatus.CANCELLED;\n    }\n\n    public OrderStatus getStatus() {\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    public int getOrderId() {\n        return orderId;\n    }\n\n    public MenuItem getItem() {\n        return item;\n    }\n\n    public int getCompletedUnits() {\n        return completedUnits;\n    }\n\n    /** Stage 1 price: read straight from the item. */\n    public int getPriceCents() {\n        return item.getPriceCents();\n    }\n\n    private void require(OrderStatus expected, String action) {\n        if (status != expected) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"The \" + item.getName() + \" order is \" + status\n                    + \"; only a \" + expected + \" order can be \" + action);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

It stays occupied until that customer pays or leaves. */\npublic class Table {\n    private final int tableId;\n    private Customer occupant;\n\n    public Table(int tableId) {\n        this.tableId = tableId;\n    }\n\n    public int getTableId() {\n        return tableId;\n    }\n\n    public boolean isAvailable() {\n        return occupant == null;\n    }\n\n    public void seat(Customer customer) {\n        if (!isAvailable()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Table \" + tableId + \" is already occupied\");\n        }\n        occupant = customer;\n    }\n\n    public void release() {\n        occupant = null;\n    }\n\n    public Customer getOccupied() {\n        return occupant;\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\n/**\n * A restaurant guest. Stage 1 has one kind of customer: a regular who starts at satisfaction 100\n * and loses 8 for every turn spent waiting. Satisfaction and status are private and change only\n * through the methods below.\n * Lifecycle: WAITING -> SEATED -> ORDERED -> SERVED -> READY_TO_PAY -> PAID, or ABANDONED.\n */\npublic class Customer {\n    private static final int MAX_SATISFACTION = 100;\n    private static final int SATISFACTION_LOSS = 8;\n\n    private final int customerId;\n    private int satisfaction = MAX_SATISFACTION;\n    private CustomerStatus status = CustomerStatus.WAITING;\n    private Order order;\n    private int servedTurn;\n    private int readyTurn;\n\n    public Customer(int customerId) {\n        this.customerId = customerId;\n    }\n\n    public int getId() {\n        return customerId;\n    }\n\n    public CustomerStatus getStatus() {\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    public int getSatisfaction() {\n        return satisfaction;\n    }\n\n    /** Documented food rule: the customer takes menu entry number (customerId mod menu size). */\n    public MenuItem chooseMenuItem(List<MenuItem> items) {\n        return items.get(customerId % items.size());\n    }\n\n    public void assignOrder(Order newOrder) {\n        require(CustomerStatus.SEATED);\n        order = newOrder;\n        status = CustomerStatus.ORDERED;\n    }\n\n    public Order getOrder() {\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    public void markSeated() {\n        require(CustomerStatus.WAITING);\n        status = CustomerStatus.SEATED;\n    }\n\n    public void markServed(int turn) {\n        require(CustomerStatus.ORDERED);\n        servedTurn = turn;\n        status = CustomerStatus.SERVED;\n    }\n\n    /** A served customer eats for one full turn: allowed from the turn after the serving turn. */\n    public boolean canFinishEating(int turn) {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.SERVED && turn > servedTurn;\n    }\n\n    public void markReadyToPay(int turn) {\n        if (!canFinishEating(turn)) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customerId + \" has not finished eating yet\");\n        }\n        readyTurn = turn;\n        status = CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY;\n    }\n\n    /** The Cashier may charge from the turn after the customer became READY_TO_PAY. */\n    public boolean canPay(int turn) {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY && turn > readyTurn;\n    }\n\n    public int getReadyTurn() {\n        return readyTurn;\n    }\n\n    public void markPaid() {\n        require(CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY);\n        status = CustomerStatus.PAID;\n    }\n\n    /** End-of-turn loss for a customer who has not been served yet; everyone else loses nothing. */\n    public void reduceSatisfaction() {\n        if (isAwaitingService()) {\n            satisfaction = Math.max(0, satisfaction - SATISFACTION_LOSS);\n        }\n    }\n\n    public boolean shouldAbandon() {\n        return isAwaitingService() && satisfaction <= 0;\n    }\n\n    public void markAbandoned() {\n        if (!isAwaitingService()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customerId + \" is \" + status + \" and cannot abandon\");\n        }\n        status = CustomerStatus.ABANDONED;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isAwaitingService() {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.WAITING || status == CustomerStatus.SEATED\n                || status == CustomerStatus.ORDERED;\n    }\n\n    private void require(CustomerStatus expected) {\n        if (status != expected) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customerId + \" is \" + status + \", not \" + expected);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\src\\restaurantrush\\entity\\Waiter.java",
  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\n/**\n * The Waiter seats customers, takes their orders and serves finished dishes. The controller lets\n * the Waiter do at most one of these per turn; each method rejects an impossible request by\n * throwing, before anything is changed.\n */\npublic class Waiter {\n\n    public void seatCustomer(Customer customer, Table table) {\n        if (customer.getStatus() != CustomerStatus.WAITING) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customer.getId() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                    + \"; only a customer in the waiting queue can be seated\");\n        }\n        if (!table.isAvailable()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Table \" + table.getTableId() + \" is already occupied\");\n        }\n        table.seat(customer);\n        customer.markSeated();\n    }\n\n    public void takeOrder(Customer customer, MenuItem item) {\n        if (customer.getStatus() != CustomerStatus.SEATED) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customer.getId() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                    + \"; only a seated customer can order\");\n        }\n        customer.assignOrder(new Order(item));\n    }\n\n    public void serveOrder(Customer customer) {\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        if (customer.getStatus() != CustomerStatus.ORDERED || order == null) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customer.getId() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                    + \"; there is no order waiting to be served\");\n        }\n        if (!order.isReady()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customer.getId() + \"'s \" + order.getItem().getName()\n                    + \" is not READY yet (\" + order.getStatus() + \", \" + order.getCompletedUnits() + \"/\"\n                    + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits() + \" units)\");\n        }\n        order.markServed();\n        customer.markServed(0);\n    }\n}\n"
}
~~~

  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\n/** The Cashier collects the bill of one READY_TO_PAY customer, records the revenue and frees the table. */\npublic class Cashier {\n\n    public void processPayment(Customer customer, Restaurant restaurant) {\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        if (customer.getStatus() != CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY || order == null) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer \" + customer.getId() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                    + \"; the Cashier can only charge a READY_TO_PAY customer\");\n        }\n        restaurant.recordPayment(order.getPriceCents());\n        customer.markPaid();\n        order.markPaid();\n        restaurant.releaseTableOf(customer);\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collections;\nimport java.util.LinkedList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Queue;\n\n/**\n * The restaurant owns its tables, staff and menu (composition) and keeps the books: the waiting\n * queue, every customer who has arrived, and the revenue. Customers are aggregated because they\n * arrive and leave.\n */\npublic class Restaurant {\n    private static final int TABLE_COUNT = 2;\n\n    private final List<Table> tables = new ArrayList<>();\n    private final List<Customer> customers = new ArrayList<>();\n    private final Queue<Customer> waitingQueue = new LinkedList<>();\n    private final Waiter waiter = new Waiter();\n    private final Chef chef = new Chef();\n    private final Cashier cashier = new Cashier();\n    private final List<MenuItem> menuItems = new ArrayList<>();\n    private int revenueCents;\n\n    public Restaurant() {\n        for (int id = 1; id <= TABLE_COUNT; id++) {\n            tables.add(new Table(id));\n        }\n        menuItems.add(new MenuItem(\"Salad\", 900, 1));\n        menuItems.add(new MenuItem(\"Burger\", 1500, 1));\n        menuItems.add(new MenuItem(\"Pasta\", 1800, 2));\n    }\n\n    /** A new regular customer joins the back of the waiting queue. */\n    public Customer registerArrival() {\n        Customer customer = new Customer(customers.size() + 1);\n        customers.add(customer);\n        waitingQueue.add(customer);\n        return customer;\n    }\n\n    /** The first free table, or null when both are occupied. */\n    public Table findAvailableTable() {\n        for (Table table : tables) {\n            if (table.isAvailable()) {\n                return table;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /** The READY_TO_PAY customer who arrived first and may be charged this turn, or null. */\n    public Customer findOldestEligiblePayer(int turn) {\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            if (customer.canPay(turn)) {\n                return customer;\n            }\n        }\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    public Waiter getWaiter() {\n        return waiter;\n    }\n\n    public Chef getChef() {\n        return chef;\n    }\n\n    public Cashier getCashier() {\n        return cashier;\n    }\n\n    public List<Table> getTables() {\n        return Collections.unmodifiableList(tables);\n    }\n\n    public List<MenuItem> getMenuItems() {\n        return Collections.unmodifiableList(menuItems);\n    }\n\n    public List<Customer> getCustomers() {\n        return Collections.unmodifiableList(customers);\n    }\n\n    /** The live FIFO queue: the controller removes a customer from it once the customer is seated. */\n    public Queue<Customer> getWaitingQueue() {\n        return waitingQueue;\n    }\n\n    public void recordPayment(int amountCents) {\n        if (amountCents < 0) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"A payment cannot be negative\");\n        }\n        revenueCents += amountCents;\n    }\n\n    public void releaseTableOf(Customer customer) {\n        for (Table table : tables) {\n            if (table.getOccupied() == customer) {\n                table.release();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    public int getPaidCustomerCount() {\n        return countWithStatus(CustomerStatus.PAID);\n    }\n\n    public int getServedCustomerCount() {\n        return countWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SERVED) + countWithStatus(CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY)\n                + countWithStatus(CustomerStatus.PAID);\n    }\n\n    /** Mean satisfaction of paid customers, as recorded when each paid; 0 when nobody has paid. */\n    public double getAverageSatisfaction() {\n        int total = 0;\n        int paid = 0;\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            if (customer.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.PAID) {\n                total += customer.getSatisfaction();\n                paid++;\n            }\n        }\n        return paid == 0 ? 0.0 : (double) total / paid;\n    }\n\n    public int getRevenueCents() {\n        return revenueCents;\n    }\n\n    /** A customer whose satisfaction hit 0 leaves: cancel the unfinished order, free the table, leave the queue. */\n    public void handleAbandonment(Customer customer) {\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        if (order != null) {\n            order.cancel();\n        }\n        releaseTableOf(customer);\n        waitingQueue.remove(customer);\n        customer.markAbandoned();\n    }\n\n    public int getUnhappyDepartureCount() {\n        return countWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ABANDONED);\n    }\n\n    private int countWithStatus(CustomerStatus status) {\n        int count = 0;\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            if (customer.getStatus() == status) {\n                count++;\n            }\n        }\n        return count;\n    }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\src\\restaurantrush\\control\\GameController.java",
  "content": "package restaurantrush.control;\n\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.Collection;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.function.BiConsumer;\nimport java.util.function.Consumer;\nimport java.util.function.Supplier;\n\nimport restaurantrush.boundary.RestaurantCLI;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.MenuItem;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Money;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Order;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Table;\n\n/**\n * Runs the game: 18 turns of six steps each (arrivals, manager choices, kitchen and serving,\n * eating and payment, waiting and abandonment, result). It owns the turn counter and decides\n * whether a choice is legal; the CLI only collects choices and displays state.\n */\npublic class GameController {\n    public static final int REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS = 4;\n    public static final int REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS = 4500;\n    public static final int REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION = 60;\n\n    private static final int MAX_TURNS = 18;\n    private static final int ARRIVAL_INTERVAL = 3;\n    private static final int LAST_ARRIVAL_TURN = 16;\n\n    private final Restaurant restaurant;\n    private int currentTurn;\n    private final int maxTurns;\n    private final RestaurantCLI cli;\n\n    public GameController(RestaurantCLI cli) {\n        this.cli = cli;\n        this.restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        this.maxTurns = MAX_TURNS;\n    }\n\n    public int getCurrentTurn() {\n        return currentTurn;\n    }\n\n    public int getMaxTurns() {\n        return maxTurns;\n    }\n\n    /** Plays all turns (there is no early ending), then reports victory or defeat. */\n    public void startGame() {\n        while (currentTurn < maxTurns) {\n            currentTurn++;\n            executeTurn();\n        }\n        cli.displayResult(evaluateGameResult(), restaurant);\n    }\n\n    private void executeTurn() {\n        processScheduledArrival();\n        cli.displayTurnStatus(restaurant);\n        coordinateStaffActions();\n        coordinatePaymentProcessing();\n        processEndOfTurnUpdates();\n    }\n\n    /** Step 1: one customer arrives at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16. */\n    private void processScheduledArrival() {\n        if (currentTurn % ARRIVAL_INTERVAL == 1 && currentTurn <= LAST_ARRIVAL_TURN) {\n            restaurant.registerArrival();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Steps 2 and 3: the Waiter acts first, then the Chef. A rejected choice does not use up the task. */\n    private void coordinateStaffActions() {\n        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readWaiterChoice, this::performWaiterTask);\n        chooseUntilAccepted(cli::readChefChoice, this::performChefTask);\n    }\n\n    private void chooseUntilAccepted(Supplier<String> reader, Consumer<String> task) {\n        while (true) {\n            String choice = reader.get();\n            try {\n                task.accept(choice);\n                return;\n            } catch (IllegalArgumentException | IllegalStateException rejected) {\n                cli.displayError(rejected.getMessage());\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void performWaiterTask(String choice) {\n        Command command = Command.parse(choice);\n        switch (command.verb) {\n            case \"WAIT\" -> { }\n            case \"SEAT\" -> seatQueuedCustomer(command, restaurant.getWaiter()::seatCustomer, \"Waiter\");\n            case \"TAKE_ORDER\", \"ORDER\", \"TAKE\" -> takeOrder(command);\n            case \"SERVE\" -> serve(command);\n            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Unknown Waiter command '\" + command.verb + \"'. Use SEAT, TAKE_ORDER, SERVE or WAIT.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void performChefTask(String choice) {\n        Command command = Command.parse(choice);\n        switch (command.verb) {\n            case \"WAIT\" -> { }\n            case \"COOK\", \"PREPARE\", \"ADVANCE\" -> prepare(command);\n            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException(\n                    \"Unknown Chef command '\" + command.verb + \"'. Use COOK or WAIT.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void seatQueuedCustomer(Command command, BiConsumer<Customer, Table> seater, String actor) {\n        Customer customer = select(command, new ArrayList<>(restaurant.getWaitingQueue()),\n                \"nobody is in the waiting queue\", \"only a customer in the waiting queue can be seated\");\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        if (table == null) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Both tables are occupied; a table frees up when its customer pays or leaves\");\n        }\n        seater.accept(customer, table);\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(customer);\n        cli.displayMessage(actor + \" seats Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \" at Table \" + table.getTableId() + \".\");\n    }\n\n    private void takeOrder(Command command) {\n        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.SEATED),\n                \"no seated customer is waiting to order\", \"only a seated customer can order\");\n        MenuItem item = customer.chooseMenuItem(restaurant.getMenuItems());\n        restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, item);\n        cli.displayMessage(\"Waiter takes Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \"'s order. They choose \"\n                + item.getName() + \" (\" + Money.format(item.getPriceCents()) + \", \"\n                + item.getPreparationUnits() + \" preparation unit(s)).\");\n    }\n\n    private void serve(Command command) {\n        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),\n                \"no customer has an order to serve\", \"only a customer who has ordered can be served\");\n        restaurant.getWaiter().serveOrder(customer, currentTurn);\n        cli.displayMessage(\"Waiter serves Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \"'s \"\n                + customer.getOrder().getItem().getName() + \".\");\n    }\n\n    private void prepare(Command command) {\n        Customer customer = select(command, customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus.ORDERED),\n                \"no order has been taken yet\", \"only a customer who has ordered has food to prepare\");\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        restaurant.getChef().prepare(order);\n        cli.displayMessage(\"Chef works on Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \"'s \" + order.getItem().getName()\n                + \": \" + order.getCompletedUnits() + \"/\" + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits()\n                + \" units, order is \" + order.getStatus() + \".\");\n    }\n\n    /** Step 4: served customers finish eating, then the Cashier charges at most one READY_TO_PAY customer. */\n    private void coordinatePaymentProcessing() {\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.canFinishEating(currentTurn)) {\n                customer.markReadyToPay(currentTurn);\n                cli.displayMessage(\"Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \" finishes eating and is ready to pay.\");\n            }\n        }\n        Customer payer = restaurant.findOldestEligiblePayer(currentTurn);\n        if (payer != null) {\n            int bill = payer.getOrder().getPriceCents();\n            restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(payer, restaurant);\n            cli.displayMessage(\"Cashier collects \" + Money.format(bill) + \" from Customer #\" + payer.getId()\n                    + \" (satisfaction \" + payer.getSatisfaction() + \"). The table is free again.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Step 5: customers who are still waiting lose satisfaction; at 0 they leave without paying. */\n    private void processEndOfTurnUpdates() {\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            customer.reduceSatisfaction();\n            if (customer.shouldAbandon()) {\n                restaurant.handleAbandonment(customer);\n                cli.displayMessage(\"Customer #\" + customer.getId()\n                        + \" runs out of patience and leaves without paying. Any unfinished order is cancelled.\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /** Step 6: victory needs all three targets. */\n    private boolean evaluateGameResult() {\n        return restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() >= REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS\n                && restaurant.getRevenueCents() >= REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS\n                && restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction() >= REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION;\n    }\n\n    /** Resolves the customer a command refers to, or explains why it cannot. */\n    private Customer select(Command command, List<Customer> candidates, String noCandidates, String ineligible) {\n        if (command.customerId == null) {\n            if (candidates.isEmpty()) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"Nothing to do: \" + noCandidates);\n            }\n            if (candidates.size() > 1) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Several customers qualify (\" + idList(candidates)\n                        + \"); add a customer number, for example \" + command.verb + \" \" + candidates.get(0).getId());\n            }\n            return candidates.get(0);\n        }\n        for (Customer candidate : candidates) {\n            if (candidate.getId() == command.customerId) {\n                return candidate;\n            }\n        }\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.getId() == command.customerId) {\n                throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer #\" + customer.getId() + \" is \" + customer.getStatus()\n                        + \": \" + ineligible);\n            }\n        }\n        throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"There is no customer #\" + command.customerId);\n    }\n\n    private List<Customer> customersWithStatus(CustomerStatus status) {\n        List<Customer> matching = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.getStatus() == status) {\n                matching.add(customer);\n            }\n        }\n        return matching;\n    }\n\n    private static String idList(Collection<Customer> customers) {\n        StringBuilder ids = new StringBuilder();\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            ids.append(ids.length() == 0 ? \"#\" : \", #\").append(customer.getId());\n        }\n        return ids.toString();\n    }\n\n    /** A manager command: a verb and an optional customer number. A blank line means WAIT. */\n    private static final class Command {\n        private final String verb;\n        private final Integer customerId;\n\n        private Command(String verb, Integer customerId) {\n            this.verb = verb;\n            this.customerId = customerId;\n        }\n\n        private static Command parse(String raw) {\n            String text = raw == null ? \"\" : raw.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            if (text.isEmpty()) {\n                return new Command(\"WAIT\", null);\n            }\n            String[] words = text.split(\"\\\\s+\");\n            if (words.length > 2) {\n                throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Expected 'COMMAND [customer number]' but got '\" + raw.trim() + \"'\");\n            }\n            Integer id = null;\n            if (words.length == 2) {\n                String number = words[1].startsWith(\"#\") ? words[1].substring(1) : words[1];\n                try {\n                    id = Integer.valueOf(number);\n                } catch (NumberFormatException notANumber) {\n                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"'\" + words[1] + \"' is not a customer number\");\n                }\n            }\n            return new Command(words[0], id);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}
~~~

  "content": "package restaurantrush.boundary;\n\nimport java.io.InputStream;\nimport java.io.PrintStream;\nimport java.util.ArrayList;\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.Locale;\nimport java.util.NoSuchElementException;\nimport java.util.Scanner;\n\nimport restaurantrush.control.GameController;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Money;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Order;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Table;\n\n/**\n * The command-line boundary. It prints the game state and reads the manager's choices as text;\n * it contains no game rules (the controller decides whether a choice is legal).\n */\npublic class RestaurantCLI {\n    private final Scanner input;\n    private final PrintStream out;\n    private final boolean echoInput;\n    private GameController controller;\n\n    /** echoInput repeats each line read, so a run fed from a file (stdin) reads like a transcript. */\n    public RestaurantCLI(InputStream in, PrintStream out, boolean echoInput) {\n        this.input = new Scanner(in);\n        this.out = out;\n        this.echoInput = echoInput;\n    }\n\n    /** Runs one game. If the input ends before the game does, the run stops with a message. */\n    public void start() {\n        controller = new GameController(this);\n        out.println(\"SC2002 Restaurant Rush - Stage 1\");\n        out.println(\"You manage a restaurant for \" + controller.getMaxTurns() + \" turns. Each turn the Waiter does one\");\n        out.println(\"task (SEAT, TAKE_ORDER or SERVE) and the Chef advances one order by one unit (COOK).\");\n        out.println(\"Press Enter to WAIT. Goal: at least \" + GameController.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + \" paid customers, revenue of at least \"\n                + Money.format(GameController.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + \", average satisfaction of at least \"\n                + GameController.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + \".\");\n        try {\n            controller.startGame();\n        } catch (NoSuchElementException inputEnded) {\n            out.println();\n            out.println(\"The input ended before the game finished, so the run stops here.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    public String readWaiterChoice() {\n        return readLine(\"Waiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: \");\n    }\n\n    public String readChefChoice() {\n        return readLine(\"Chef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]: \");\n    }\n\n    public void displayTurnStatus(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        out.println();\n        out.println(\"=================== TURN \" + controller.getCurrentTurn() + \" of \" + controller.getMaxTurns()\n                + \" ===================\");\n        out.println(\"Revenue: \" + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents())\n                + \" | Average satisfaction of paid customers: \" + averageText(restaurant)\n                + \" | Served: \" + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()\n                + \" | Unhappy departures: \" + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount());\n        out.println(\"Waiting queue: \" + queueText(restaurant));\n        out.println(\"Tables: \" + tablesText(restaurant));\n        out.println(\"Customers:\");\n        if (restaurant.getCustomers().isEmpty()) {\n            out.println(\"  (nobody has arrived yet)\");\n        }\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            out.println(String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"  %-4s satisfaction %3d  %-12s order: %s\",\n                    \"#\" + customer.getId(), customer.getSatisfaction(), customer.getStatus(), orderText(customer)));\n        }\n        out.println(\"Waiter tasks: \" + waiterTasksText(restaurant));\n        out.println(\"Chef tasks:   \" + chefTasksText(restaurant));\n    }\n\n    public void displayMessage(String message) {\n        out.println(\"  * \" + message);\n    }\n\n    public void displayError(String message) {\n        out.println(\"  ! Not possible: \" + message + \". Choose again.\");\n    }\n\n    public void displayResult(boolean won, Restaurant restaurant) {\n        out.println();\n        out.println(\"=================== GAME OVER ===================\");\n        out.println(won ? \"VICTORY\" : \"DEFEAT\");\n        out.println(\"Paid customers: \" + restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() + \" (need \"\n                + GameController.REQUIRED_PAID_CUSTOMERS + \")\");\n        out.println(\"Revenue: \" + Money.format(restaurant.getRevenueCents()) + \" (need \"\n                + Money.format(GameController.REQUIRED_REVENUE_CENTS) + \")\");\n        out.println(\"Average satisfaction of paid customers: \" + averageText(restaurant) + \" (need \"\n                + GameController.REQUIRED_AVERAGE_SATISFACTION + \")\");\n        out.println(\"Customers served: \" + restaurant.getServedCustomerCount()\n                + \" | Unhappy departures: \" + restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount()\n                + \" | Turns used: \" + controller.getCurrentTurn());\n    }\n\n    private String readLine(String prompt) {\n        out.print(prompt);\n        out.flush();\n        String line = input.nextLine();\n        if (echoInput) {\n            out.println(line);\n        }\n        return line;\n    }\n\n    private static String averageText(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        if (restaurant.getPaidCustomerCount() == 0) {\n            return \"N/A\";\n        }\n        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"%.1f\", restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction());\n    }\n\n    private static String queueText(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        if (restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty()) {\n            return \"empty\";\n        }\n        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getWaitingQueue()) {\n            entries.add(\"#\" + customer.getId() + \" (satisfaction \" + customer.getSatisfaction() + \")\");\n        }\n        return String.join(\" -> \", entries);\n    }\n\n    private static String tablesText(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        List<String> entries = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Table table : restaurant.getTables()) {\n            Customer occupant = table.getOccupied();\n            entries.add(\"Table \" + table.getTableId() + \": \"\n                    + (occupant == null ? \"free\" : \"#\" + occupant.getId() + \" (\" + occupant.getStatus() + \")\"));\n        }\n        return String.join(\" | \", entries);\n    }\n\n    private static String orderText(Customer customer) {\n        Order order = customer.getOrder();\n        if (order == null) {\n            return \"-\";\n        }\n        return order.getItem().getName() + \" \" + order.getCompletedUnits() + \"/\"\n                + order.getItem().getPreparationUnits() + \" units, \" + order.getStatus()\n                + \", \" + Money.format(order.getPriceCents());\n    }\n\n    private static String waiterTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        List<String> tasks = new ArrayList<>();\n        if (!restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty() && restaurant.findAvailableTable() != null) {\n            tasks.add(\"SEAT \" + ids(new ArrayList<>(restaurant.getWaitingQueue())));\n        }\n        List<Customer> seated = withStatus(restaurant, CustomerStatus.SEATED, false);\n        if (!seated.isEmpty()) {\n            tasks.add(\"TAKE_ORDER \" + ids(seated));\n        }\n        List<Customer> ready = withStatus(restaurant, CustomerStatus.ORDERED, true);\n        if (!ready.isEmpty()) {\n            tasks.add(\"SERVE \" + ids(ready));\n        }\n        return tasks.isEmpty() ? \"none available (WAIT)\" : String.join(\"; \", tasks);\n    }\n\n    private static String chefTasksText(Restaurant restaurant) {\n        List<Customer> cooking = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : withStatus(restaurant, CustomerStatus.ORDERED, false)) {\n            if (!customer.getOrder().isReady()) {\n                cooking.add(customer);\n            }\n        }\n        return cooking.isEmpty() ? \"none available (WAIT)\" : \"COOK \" + ids(cooking);\n    }\n\n    /** Customers with the given status; for ORDERED, readyOnly keeps those whose dish is READY. */\n    private static List<Customer> withStatus(Restaurant restaurant, CustomerStatus status, boolean readyOnly) {\n        List<Customer> matching = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {\n            if (customer.getStatus() == status && (!readyOnly || customer.getOrder().isReady())) {\n                matching.add(customer);\n            }\n        }\n        return matching;\n    }\n\n    private static String ids(List<Customer> customers) {\n        List<String> numbers = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (Customer customer : customers) {\n            numbers.add(\"#\" + customer.getId());\n        }\n        return String.join(\" \", numbers);\n    }\n}\n"
}

    customerChoosesFoodByIdRule();\n        tableHoldsOneCustomer();\n        paymentRequiresReadyToPayAndFreesTable();\n        abandonmentCancelsOrderFreesTableAndLeavesQueue();\n available\", !table.isAvailable() && table.getOccupied() == first);\n        expectRejected(\"a second customer cannot take an occupied table\", () -> table.seat(new Customer(2)));\n        table.release();\n        check(\"released table is available\", table.isAvailable());\n    }\n\n    private static void paymentRequiresReadyToPayAndFreesTable() {\n        Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        Customer customer = restaurant.registerArrival();\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        restaurant.getWaiter().seatCustomer(customer, table);\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(customer);\n        expectRejected(\"the Cashier cannot charge a seated customer\",\n                () -> restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant));\n        restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, customer.chooseMenuItem(restaurant.getMenuItems()));\n        restaurant.getChef().prepare(customer.getOrder());\n        restaurant.getWaiter().serveOrder(customer, 3);\n        customer.markReadyToPay(4);\n        restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant);\n        check(\"payment adds the bill to revenue\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 1500);\n        check(\"customer and order are PAID\", customer.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.PAID\n                && customer.getOrder().getStatus() == OrderStatus.PAID);\n        check(\"the table is free again\", table.isAvailable());\n        check(\"average satisfaction counts the paid customer\", restaurant.getAverageSatisfaction() == 100.0);\n        expectRejected(\"paying twice is rejected\", () -> restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant));\n        check(\"revenue is unchanged after the rejected second payment\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 1500);\n    }\n\n    private static void abandonmentCancelsOrderFreesTableAndLeavesQueue() {\n        Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        Customer seated = restaurant.registerArrival();\n        Customer queued = restaurant.registerArrival();\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        restaurant.getWaiter().seatCustomer(seated, table);\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(seated);\n        restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(seated, seated.chooseMenuItem(restaurant.getMenuItems()));\n        restaurant.handleAbandonment(seated);\n        check(\"abandoned customer is ABANDONED\", seated.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.ABANDONED);\n        check(\"abandoned customer's order is CANCELLED\", seated.getOrder().getStatus() == OrderStatus.CANCELLED);\n        check(\"abandoned customer's table is free\", table.isAvailable());\n        restaurant.handleAbandonment(queued);\n        check(\"a queued customer who leaves is removed from the queue\", restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty());\n        check(\"two unhappy departures are counted\", restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount() == 2);\n        check(\"abandoned customers add no revenue\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 0);\n    }\n\n    // ---- full-game checks: replay the recorded inputs in runs/ ---------------------------\n\n    private static void victoryRunIsReproducible() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = play(\"runs/victory.txt\");\n        check(\"victory run ends in VICTORY\", transcript.contains(\"\\nVICTORY\\n\"));\n        check(\"victory run: 4 paid\", transcript.contains(\"Paid customers: 4 (need 4)\"));\n        check(\"victory run: $57.00\", transcript.contains(\"Revenue: $57.00 (need $45.00)\"));\n        check(\"victory run: average 78.0\", transcript.contains(\"Average satisfaction of paid customers: 78.0\"));\n        check(\"the game ran all 18 turns\", transcript.contains(\"Turns used: 18\"));\n        check(\"exactly six customers arrive in the base setting\", transcript.contains(\"  #6 \") && !transcript.contains(\"  #7 \"));\n    }\n\n    private static void defeatRunIsReproducible() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = play(\"runs/defeat.txt\");\n        check(\"defeat run ends in DEFEAT\", transcript.contains(\"\\nDEFEAT\\n\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: nobody paid, revenue $0.00\", transcript.contains(\"Paid customers: 0\")\n                && transcript.contains(\"Revenue: $0.00 (need\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: average shows N/A\", transcript.contains(\"Average satisfaction of paid customers: N/A (need\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: two customers walked out\", transcript.contains(\"Unhappy departures: 2\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void invalidChoicesDoNotUseUpTheTask() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = play(\"runs/victory.txt\");\n        check(\"an unknown customer is explained\", transcript.contains(\"There is no customer #9\"));\n        check(\"serving a dish that is not READY is explained\", transcript.contains(\"is not READY yet\"));\n        check(\"a rejected choice is followed by another prompt in the same turn\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Choose again.\\nWaiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: \")\n                        || transcript.contains(\"Choose again.\\nChef    [COOK <#> | WAIT]: \"));\n    }\n\n    // ---- tiny helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static String play(String inputFile) throws IOException {\n        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        try (FileInputStream choices = new FileInputStream(inputFile);\n                PrintStream out = new PrintStream(captured, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n            new RestaurantCLI(choices, out, true).start();\n        }\n        return captured.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).replace(\"\\r\\n\", \"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    private static boolean item(List<MenuItem> menu, String name, int priceCents, int units) {\n        for (MenuItem candidate : menu) {\n            if (candidate.getName().equals(name)) {\n                return candidate.getPriceCents() == priceCents && candidate.getPreparationUnits() == units;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static void expectRejected(String name, Runnable action) {\n        try {\n            action.run();\n            check(name, false);\n        } catch (IllegalStateException | IllegalArgumentException expected) {\n            check(name, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void check(String name, boolean condition) {\n        if (condition) {\n            passed++;\n        } else {\n            failed++;\n            System.out.println(\"FAILED: \" + name);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

With no arguments the manager types the choices. With \"--input choices.txt\" the\n * choices are read from a file instead (and echoed), which replays a recorded run.\n */\npublic final class Main {\n    private Main() {\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        if (args.length == 0) {\n            new RestaurantCLI(System.in, System.out, false).start();\n        } else if (args.length == 2 && args[0].equals(\"--input\")) {\n            try (InputStream choices = new FileInputStream(args[1])) {\n                new RestaurantCLI(choices, System.out, true).start();\n            } catch (IOException unreadable) {\n                System.err.println(\"Cannot read the choices file '\" + args[1] + \"': \" + unreadable.getMessage());\n                System.exit(1);\n            }\n        } else {\n            System.err.println(\"Usage: java -cp out restaurantrush.Main [--input choices.txt]\");\n            System.exit(2);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

### Tool call: Write (toolu_01QtNsbgXRjE92PAghBAHLR8)

~~~json
{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\README.md",
  "content": "# SC2002 Restaurant Rush\n\nA turn-based restaurant management game for the command line, written in Java for the SC2002 group project.\nThe player is the manager: staff time is limited, so the manager decides which customer or order comes first.\n\nRepository: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject\n\n## Build and run\n\nNeeds JDK 17 or newer. No other dependencies, no network. Run every command from the repository root.\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main\n```\n\nReplay a recorded run instead of typing the choices (the file is read and echoed, so the screen shows a transcript):\n\n```\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt\n```\n\nRun the automated checks (see [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md) for what they cover and for the expected results of both runs):\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests\n```\n\n## Stage tags\n\nEach stage is a separate tagged commit that builds and runs on its own.\n\n| Tag | Adds |\n|---|---|\n| `stage-1` | Basic operation: Regular customers, menu, Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Table, Order, the waiting queue, the full six-step turn, abandonment, victory and defeat |\n\n## How to play (Stage 1)\n\nThe base setting is fixed: 18 turns, 2 tables, one Waiter, one Chef, one Cashier, and one new customer at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16.\n\n| Item | Price | Preparation units |\n|---|---|---|\n| Salad | $9.00 | 1 |\n| Burger | $15.00 | 1 |\n| Pasta | $18.00 | 2 |\n\nEvery turn the screen shows the turn number, revenue, the average satisfaction of paid customers, the waiting queue, the tables, every customer's satisfaction and order status, and the tasks the staff can do. Then you decide:\n\n| Prompt | Commands | What happens |\n|---|---|---|\n| Waiter (one task per turn) | `SEAT <#>`, `TAKE_ORDER <#>`, `SERVE <#>`, `WAIT` | seat a queued customer at a free table, take a seated customer's order, or serve a READY order |\n| Chef (one unit per turn) | `COOK <#>`, `WAIT` | advance a customer's order by one preparation unit |\n\n`<#>` is the customer number shown on screen (`#2` and `2` both work). It may be left out when only one customer qualifies. A blank line means `WAIT`. A choice that cannot be done is explained and you choose again; it does not use up the staff task.\n\nAfter your choices the turn finishes on its own: customers who were served earlier finish eating (READY_TO_PAY), the Cashier charges at most one customer who became READY_TO_PAY on an earlier turn, and every customer who is still waiting for service loses 8 satisfaction. A customer who reaches 0 leaves without paying, the order is cancelled and the table is freed.\n\nAfter turn 18 you win if at least 4 customers have paid, revenue is at least $45.00 and the average satisfaction of the customers who paid is at least 60. Otherwise you lose. The game never ends early.\n\nRules this implementation fixes where the brief leaves a choice:\n\n- **Food choice.** A customer takes menu entry number `customerId mod 3` (Salad is 0, Burger 1, Pasta 2), so customer 1 orders a Burger, 2 a Pasta, 3 a Salad, 4 a Burger, and so on. The manager never picks the food; the choice is shown when the order is taken.\n- **Order of events in a turn.** Arrival, your Waiter choice, your Chef choice (so the Chef can work on an order taken this turn), eating and payment, then waiting and abandonment. A dish the Chef finishes this turn is READY only after the Waiter has acted, so it is served next turn at the earliest.\n- **Eating and payment.** A customer served on turn *n* is READY_TO_PAY on turn *n*+1 and can pay on turn *n*+2. \"Oldest first\" means the customer who arrived first.\n\n## Repository layout\n\n| Path | Contents |\n|---|---|\n| `src/restaurantrush/` | Java source. `entity/` holds the domain classes, `control/` the turn controller, `boundary/` the command-line interface |\n| `test/restaurantrush/` | `GameTests`, plain-Java checks |\n| `runs/` | Recorded manager choices that replay a victory and a defeat |\n| `docs/` | Assignment brief and briefing, test notes, UML diagrams (`docs/diagrams`, sources in `docs/diagrams/src`) |\n| `docs/individual/<member>/` | Each member's `reflection/` and `chat-history/` folders |\n\nThe `individual` folders are directories in this repository, not Git branches.\n"
}
~~~

  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\docs\\test-notes.md",
  "content": "# Test notes\n\nRun every command from the repository root with JDK 17 or newer. Build first:\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java\n```\n\n## Stage 1 (tag `stage-1`)\n\n### Automated checks\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests\n```\n\nExpected: `55 checks passed, 0 failed`. The checks cover:\n\n- the menu (prices and preparation units from the brief);\n- legal and illegal order transitions (a 1-unit dish goes PLACED to READY, a 2-unit dish goes through PREPARING, serving a dish that is not READY is rejected, a PAID order cannot be cancelled);\n- the Regular customer's satisfaction (8 lost per waiting turn while waiting, seated or ordered; nothing lost once served; 0 is the floor and triggers abandonment);\n- the turn guards (eating needs a turn after serving, payment needs a turn after READY_TO_PAY);\n- the food rule, tables, payment (revenue, PAID customer and order, table freed, no double payment) and abandonment (order cancelled, table freed, queue left);\n- the two recorded runs below, including that exactly six customers arrive and that all 18 turns are played.\n\n### Reproducible victory run\n\n```\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt\n```\n\nExpected ending: `VICTORY`, 4 paid customers, revenue $57.00, average satisfaction 78.0, 5 customers served, 0 unhappy departures, 18 turns used.\n\n| Turn | Waiter | Chef | What to look for |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | SEAT 1 | WAIT | #1 seated, satisfaction 100 to 92 |\n| 2 | SERVE 1 (rejected), TAKE_ORDER 1 | COOK 1 | rejected choice is explained and the Waiter chooses again; Burger is READY at the end of the turn |\n| 3 | SERVE 1 | WAIT | READY to SERVED on a later turn; #1 stops losing satisfaction (84) |\n| 4 | SEAT 2 | WAIT | #2 arrives; #1 finishes eating and is READY_TO_PAY |\n| 5 | TAKE_ORDER 2 | COOK 2 | #2 orders Pasta (2 units, 1/2 done); Cashier collects $15.00 from #1 |\n| 6 | SERVE 2 (rejected), WAIT | COOK 1 (rejected), COOK 2 | the Pasta is only PREPARING, so it cannot be served; it becomes READY |\n| 7 | SERVE 2 | WAIT | #3 arrives |\n| 8 | SEAT 3 | WAIT | #2 is READY_TO_PAY |\n| 9 | TAKE_ORDER 3 | COOK 3 | Cashier collects $18.00 from #2 |\n| 10 | SERVE 3 | WAIT | #4 arrives |\n| 11 | SEAT 9 (rejected), SEAT 4 | WAIT | \"There is no customer #9\"; #3 is READY_TO_PAY |\n| 12 | TAKE_ORDER 4 | COOK 4 | Cashier collects $9.00 from #3 |\n| 13 | SERVE 4 | WAIT | #5 arrives |\n| 14 | SEAT 5 | WAIT | #4 is READY_TO_PAY |\n| 15 | TAKE_ORDER 5 | COOK 5 | Cashier collects $15.00 from #4 (4th payment) |\n| 16 | WAIT | COOK 5 | #6 arrives (the turn-16 customer cannot finish paying in time) |\n| 17 | SERVE 5 | WAIT | |\n| 18 | WAIT | WAIT | #5 becomes READY_TO_PAY too late to pay;   "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\n/**\n * A restaurant guest. Every kind of customer follows the same order and service flow; subclasses\n * differ only in the hooks below (loss per waiting turn, billing, food choice, reaction to being\n * served), so the controller and staff call these on Customer and never check the type.\n * Satisfaction and status are private and change only through the methods of this class.\n * Lifecycle: WAITING -> SEATED -> ORDERED -> SERVED -> READY_TO_PAY -> PAID, or ABANDONED.\n */\npublic abstract class Customer {\n    private static final int MAX_SATISFACTION = 100;\n\n    private final int customerId;\n    private int satisfaction = MAX_SATISFACTION;\n    private CustomerStatus status = CustomerStatus.WAITING;\n    private Order order;\n    private int servedTurn;\n    private int readyTurn;\n\n    protected Customer(int customerId) {\n        this.customerId = customerId;\n    }\n\n    public int getId() {\n        return customerId;\n    }\n\n    public CustomerStatus getStatus() {\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    public int getSatisfaction() {\n        return satisfaction;\n    }\n\n    /** Short name of the customer type, for display. */\n    public abstract String getTypeLabel();\n\n    /** Satisfaction lost at the end of each turn spent waiting for service. */\n    protected abstract int getSatisfactionLoss();\n\n    /** Documented food rule: menu entry number (customerId mod menu size). The Critic overrides it. */\n    public MenuItem chooseMenuItem(Menu menu) {\n        return menu.getItem(customerId % menu.size());\n    }\n\n    /** The bill for a dish at the given list price. The VIP overrides it to apply a discount. */\n    public int calculateBillCents(int basePriceCents) {\n        return basePriceCents;\n    }\n\n    /** Called when the Waiter serves this customer; turnsWaited counts turns since the order was taken. */\n    public void onServed(int turnsWaited) {\n    }\n\n    /** Only subclasses may move satisfaction directly; the value always stays within 0..100. */\n    protected void adjustSatisfaction(int delta) {\n        satisfaction = Math.max(0, Math.min(MAX_SATISFACTION, satisfaction + delta));\n    }\n\n    /** End-of-turn loss for a customer who has not been served yet; everyone else loses nothing. */\n    public void reduceSatisfaction() {\n        if (isAwaitingService()) {\n            adjustSatisfaction(-getSatisfactionLoss());\n        }\n    }\n\n    public void assignOrder(Order newOrder) {\n        require(CustomerStatus.SEATED);\n        order = newOrder;\n        status = CustomerStatus.ORDERED;\n    }\n\n    public Order getOrder() {\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    public void markSeated() {\n        require(CustomerStatus.WAITING);\n        status = CustomerStatus.SEATED;\n    }\n\n    public void markServed(int turn) {\n        require(CustomerStatus.ORDERED);\n        servedTurn = turn;\n        status = CustomerStatus.SERVED;\n    }\n\n    /** A served customer eats for one full turn: allowed from the turn after the serving turn. */\n    public boolean canFinishEating(int turn) {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.SERVED && turn > servedTurn;\n    }\n\n    public void markReadyToPay(int turn) {\n        if (!canFinishEating(turn)) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer #\" + customerId + \" has not finished eating yet\");\n        }\n        readyTurn = turn;\n        status = CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY;\n    }\n\n    /** The Cashier may charge from the turn after the customer became READY_TO_PAY. */\n    public boolean canPay(int turn) {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY && turn > readyTurn;\n    }\n\n    public void markPaid() {\n        require(CustomerStatus.READY_TO_PAY);\n        status = CustomerStatus.PAID;\n    }\n\n    public boolean shouldAbandon() {\n        return isAwaitingService() && satisfaction <= 0;\n    }\n\n    public void markAbandoned() {\n        if (!isAwaitingService()) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer #\" + customerId + \" is \" + status + \" and cannot abandon\");\n        }\n        status = CustomerStatus.ABANDONED;\n    }\n\n    private boolean isAwaitingService() {\n        return status == CustomerStatus.WAITING || status == CustomerStatus.SEATED\n                || status == CustomerStatus.ORDERED;\n    }\n\n    private void require(CustomerStatus expected) {\n        if (status != expected) {\n            throw new IllegalStateException(\"Customer #\" + customerId + \" is \" + status + \", not \" + expected);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package restaurantrush.entity;\n\n/**\n * A demanding customer: loses 12 per waiting turn, always orders the most expensive dish, and is\n * further upset (-10) when served more than 2 turns after the order was taken.\n */\npublic class CriticCustomer extends Customer {\n    private static final int SATISFACTION_LOSS = 12;\n    private static final int LATE_SERVICE_TURNS = 2;\n    private static final int LATE_PENALTY = 10;\n\n    public CriticCustomer(int customerId) {\n        super(customerId);\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public String getTypeLabel() {\n        return \"Critic\";\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    protected int getSatisfactionLoss() {\n        return SATISFACTION_LOSS;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public MenuItem chooseMenuItem(Menu menu) {\n        return menu.getMostExpensive();\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void onServed(int turnsWaited) {\n        if (turnsWaited > LATE_SERVICE_TURNS) {\n            adjustSatisfaction(-LATE_PENALTY);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "content": "package restaurantrush.control;\n\nimport java.util.List;\nimport java.util.function.IntFunction;\n\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CriticCustomer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.RegularCustomer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.VIPCustomer;\n\n/**\n * The base-setting arrivals: one customer at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16. Customers\n * are numbered in arrival order. Two of the six are special: the turn-1 arrival is a VIP and the\n * turn-10 arrival is a Critic; the other four are Regulars.\n */\npublic class ArrivalSchedule {\n    private final int[] arrivalTurns = {1, 4, 7, 10, 13, 16};\n    private final List<IntFunction<Customer>> customerTypes = List.of(\n            VIPCustomer::new, RegularCustomer::new, RegularCustomer::new,\n            CriticCustomer::new, RegularCustomer::new, RegularCustomer::new);\n    private int nextCustomerId = 1;\n\n    public boolean isArrivalTurn(int turn) {\n        return indexOf(turn) >= 0;\n    }\n\n    /** Creates the customer who arrives at the start of this turn. */\n    public Customer createArrival(int turn) {\n        int index = indexOf(turn);\n        if (index < 0) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"Nobody arrives at the start of turn \" + turn);\n        }\n        return customerTypes.get(index).apply(nextCustomerId++);\n    }\n\n    private int indexOf(int turn) {\n        for (int i = 0; i < arrivalTurns.length; i++) {\n            if (arrivalTurns[i] == turn) {\n                return i;\n            }\n        }\n        return -1;\n    }\n}\n"
}

158	    private void coordinatePaymentProcessing() {
159	        for (Customer customer : restaurant.getCustomers()) {
160	            if (customer.canFinishEating(currentTurn)) {
161	                customer.markReadyToPay(currentTurn);
162	                cli.displayMessage("Customer #" + customer.getId() + " finishes eating and is ready to pay.");
163	            }
164	        }

170	                    + " (satisfaction " + payer.getSatisfaction() + "). The table is free again.");
171	        }
172	    }

175	    private void processEndOfTurnUpdates() {

!controller.contains(\"getTypeLabel().equals\")\n                && !controller.contains(\"Critic\") && !controller.contains(\"VIP\"));\n    }\n\n    // ---- full-game checks: replay the recorded inputs in runs/ ---------------------------\n\n    private static void victoryRunIsReproducible() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = play(\"runs/victory.txt\");\n        check(\"victory DEFEAT\", transcript.contains(\"\\nDEFEAT\\n\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: only the VIP paid\", transcript.contains(\"Paid customers: 1 (need 4)\")\n                && transcript.contains(\"Customer #4 (Critic) runs out of patience\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void invalidChoicesDoNotUseUpTheTask() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = play(\"runs/victory.txt\");\n        check(\"a seat request with no free table is explained\", transcript.contains(\"Both tables are occupied\"));\n        check(\"a rejected choice is followed by another prompt in the same turn\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Choose again.\\nWaiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: \"));\n    }\n\n    // ---- tiny helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static String play(String inputFile) throws IOException {\n        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        try (FileInputStream choices = new FileInputStream(inputFile);\n                PrintStream out = new PrintStream(captured, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n            new RestaurantCLI(choices, out, true).start();\n        }\n        return captured.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).replace(\"\\r\\n\", \"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    private static boolean item(Menu menu, String name, int priceCents, int units) {\n        for (MenuItem candidate : menu.getItems()) {\n            if (candidate.getName().equals(name)) {\n                return candidate.getPriceCents() == priceCents && candidate.getPreparationUnits() == units;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static void expectRejected(String name, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void check(String name, boolean condition) {\n        if (condition) {\n            passed++;\n        } else {\n            failed++;\n   "content": "package restaurantrush;\n\nimport java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;\nimport java.io.FileInputStream;\nimport java.io.IOException;\nimport java.io.PrintStream;\nimport java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;\nimport java.nio.file.Files;\nimport java.nio.file.Path;\n\nimport restaurantrush.boundary.RestaurantCLI;\nimport restaurantrush.control.ArrivalSchedule;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CriticCustomer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Customer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.CustomerStatus;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Menu;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.MenuItem;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Order;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.OrderStatus;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.RegularCustomer;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Restaurant;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.Table;\nimport restaurantrush.entity.VIPCustomer;\n\n/**\n * Plain-Java checks (no test framework needed). The first group re-checks the Stage 1 rules that must\n * still hold; the second group covers what Stage 2 adds.\n */\npublic final class GameTests {\n    private static int passed;\n    private
