menuMatchesTheBrief();\n        orderMovesThroughLegalStatesOnly();\n        regularCustomerLosesEightPerWaitingTurnAndNothingOnceServed();\n        customerTurnGuardsDelayEatingAndPayment();\n        regularCustomerChoosesFoodByIdRule();\n        tableHoldsOneCustomer();\n        paymentRequiresReadyToPayAndFreesTable();\n        abandonmentCancelsOrderFreesTableAndLeavesQueue();\n        // Stage 2: VIP and Critic\n        mostExpensiveItemIsPasta();\n        lossPerWaitingTurnDependsOnTheCustomerType();\n        vipPaysNinetyPercentAndOthersFullPrice();\n        waiterLocksTheBillWhenTheOrderIsTaken();\n        criticAlwaysOrdersTheMostExpensiveDish();\n        criticIsUpsetWhenServedMoreThanTwoTurnsAfterOrdering();\n        scheduleHasSixArrivalsIncludingVipAndCritic();\n        controllerDispatchesThroughCustomersNotTypeChecks();\n        // Full recorded runs\n        victoryRunIsReproducible();\n        defeatRunIsReproducible();\n        invalidChoicesDoNotUseUpTheTask();\n        System.out.println(passed + \" checks passed, \" + failed + \" failed\");\n        System.exit(failed == 0 ? 0 : 1);\n    }\n\n    // ---- Stage 1 rules, re-checked --------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void menuMatchesTheBrief() {\n        Menu menu = new Restaurant().getMenu();\n        check(\"menu has three items\", menu.size() == 3);\n        check(\"Salad $9.00, 1 unit\", item(menu, \"Salad\", 900, 1));\n        check(\"Burger $15.00, 1 unit\", item(menu, \"Burger\", 1500, 1));\n        check(\"Pasta $18.00, 2 units\", item(menu, \"Pasta\", 1800, 2));\n    }\n\n    private static void orderMovesThroughLegalStatesOnly() {\n        Order order = new Order(new MenuItem(\"Pasta\", 1800, 2), 1800, 1);\n        check(\"new order is PLACED\", order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.PLACED);\n        expectRejected(\"a PLACED order cannot be served\", order::markServed);\n        order.advancePreparation();\n        check(\"first of two units: PREPARING\", order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.PREPARING);\n        order.advancePreparation();\n        check(\"last unit: READY\", order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.READY && order.isReady());\n        expectRejected(\"a READY order cannot be prepared further\", order::advancePreparation);\n        order.markServed();\n        order.markPaid();\n        check(\"served then paid: PAID\", order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.PAID);\n        expectRejected(\"a PAID order cannot be cancelled\", order::cancel);\n\n        Order burger = new Order(new MenuItem(\"Burger\", 1500, 1), 1500, 1);\n        burger.advancePreparation();\n        check(\"a 1-unit dish goes PLACED to READY\", burger.isReady());\n    }\n\n    private static void regularCustomerLosesEightPerWaitingTurnAndNothingOnceServed() {\n        Customer waiting = new RegularCustomer(1);\n        waiting.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"waiting customer loses 8\", waiting.getSatisfaction() == 92);\n        waiting.markSeated();\n        waiting.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"seated customer loses 8\", waiting.getSatisfaction() == 84);\n        waiting.assignOrder(new Order(new MenuItem(\"Burger\", 1500, 1), 1500, 2));\n        waiting.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"customer with a placed order still loses 8\", waiting.getSatisfaction() == 76);\n        waiting.markServed(5);\n        waiting.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"served customer loses nothing\", waiting.getSatisfaction() == 76);\n\n        Customer patient = new RegularCustomer(2);\n        for (int turn = 1; turn <= 12; turn++) {\n            patient.reduceSatisfaction();\n        }\n        check(\"after 12 waiting turns the customer has 4 left and stays\",\n                patient.getSatisfaction() == 4 && !patient.shouldAbandon());\n        patient.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"satisfaction bottoms out at 0, then the customer abandons\",\n                patient.getSatisfaction() == 0 && patient.shouldAbandon());\n    }\n\n    private static void customerTurnGuardsDelayEatingAndPayment() {\n        Customer customer = new RegularCustomer(1);\n        customer.markSeated();\n        customer.assignOrder(new Order(new MenuItem(\"Burger\", 1500, 1), 1500, 2));\n        customer.markServed(4);\n        check(\"cannot finish eating on the serving turn\", !customer.canFinishEating(4));\n        check(\"can finish eating a turn later\", customer.canFinishEating(5));\n        customer.markReadyToPay(5);\n        check(\"cannot pay on the READY_TO_PAY turn\", !customer.canPay(5));\n        check(\"can pay a turn later\", customer.canPay(6));\n        expectRejected(\"marking READY_TO_PAY twice is rejected\", () -> customer.markReadyToPay(6));\n    }\n\n    private static void regularCustomerChoosesFoodByIdRule() {\n        Menu menu = new Restaurant().getMenu();\n        check(\"customer 1 picks Burger\", new RegularCustomer(1).chooseMenuItem(menu).getName().equals(\"Burger\"));\n        check(\"customer 2 picks Pasta\", new RegularCustomer(2).chooseMenuItem(menu).getName().equals(\"Pasta\"));\n        check(\"customer 3 picks Salad\", new RegularCustomer(3).chooseMenuItem(menu).getName().equals(\"Salad\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void tableHoldsOneCustomer() {\n        Table table = new Table(1);\n        Customer first = new RegularCustomer(1);\n        table.seat(first);\n        check(\"seated table is not available\", !table.isAvailable() && table.getOccupied() == first);\n        expectRejected(\"a second customer cannot take an occupied table\", () -> table.seat(new RegularCustomer(2)));\n        table.release();\n        check(\"released table is available\", table.isAvailable());\n    }\n\n    private static void paymentRequiresReadyToPayAndFreesTable() {\n        Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        Customer customer = new RegularCustomer(1);\n        restaurant.registerArrival(customer);\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        restaurant.getWaiter().seatCustomer(customer, table);\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(customer);\n        expectRejected(\"the Cashier cannot charge a seated customer\",\n                () -> restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant));\n        restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, restaurant.getMenu(), 2);\n        restaurant.getChef().prepare(customer.getOrder());\n        restaurant.getWaiter().serveOrder(customer, 3);\n        customer.markReadyToPay(4);\n        restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant);\n        check(\"payment adds the bill to revenue\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 1500);\n        check(\"customer and order are PAID\", customer.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.PAID\n                && customer.getOrder().getStatus() == OrderStatus.PAID);\n        check(\"the table is free again\", restaurant.getCashier().processPayment(customer, restaurant));\n        check(\"revenue is unchanged after the rejected second payment\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 1500);\n    }\n\n    private static void abandonmentCancelsOrderFreesTableAndLeavesQueue() {\n        Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        Customer seated = new RegularCustomer(1);\n        Customer queued = new RegularCustomer(2);\n        restaurant.registerArrival(seated);\n        restaurant.registerArrival(queued);\n        Table table = restaurant.findAvailableTable();\n        restaurant.getWaiter().seatCustomer(seated, table);\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(seated);\n        restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(seated, restaurant.getMenu(), 2);\n        restaurant.handleAbandonment(seated);\n        check(\"abandoned customer is ABANDONED\", seated.getStatus() == CustomerStatus.ABANDONED);\n        check(\"abandoned customer's order is CANCELLED\", seated.getOrder().getStatus() == OrderStatus.CANCELLED);\n        check(\"abandoned customer's table is free\", table.isAvailable());\n        restaurant.handleAbandonment(queued);\n        check(\"a queued customer who leaves is removed from the queue\", restaurant.getWaitingQueue().isEmpty());\n        check(\"two unhappy departures are counted\", restaurant.getUnhappyDepartureCount() == 2);\n        check(\"abandoned customers add no revenue\", restaurant.getRevenueCents() == 0);\n    }\n\n    // ---- Stage 2: VIP and Critic ----------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static void mostExpensiveItemIsPasta() {\n        check(\"the most expensive menu item is Pasta\", new Restaurant().getMenu().getMostExpensive().getName().equals(\"Pasta\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void lossPerWaitingTurnDependsOnTheCustomerType() {\n        // Each is used only through the Customer type, as the controller does.\n        Customer[] customers = {new RegularCustomer(1), new VIPCustomer(2), new CriticCustomer(3)};\n        int[] expectedSatisfaction = {92, 95, 88};\n        for (int i = 0; i < customers.length; i++) {\n critic.reduceSatisfaction();\n        }\n        check(\"a Critic still has 4 after 8 waiting turns\", critic.getSatisfaction() == 4 && !critic.shouldAbandon());\n        critic.reduceSatisfaction();\n        check(\"a Critic reaches 0 on the 9th waiting turn and abandons\", critic.getSatisfaction() == 0 && critic.shouldAbandon());\n    }\n\n    private static void menu, String name, int priceCents, int units) {\n        for (MenuItem candidate : menu.getItems()) {\n            if (candidate.getName().equals(name)) {\n                return candidate.getPriceCents() == priceCents && candidate.getPreparationUnits() == units;\n            }\n        }\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    private static void expectRejected(String name, Runnable action) {\n        try {\n            action.run();\n            check(name, false);\n        } catch (IllegalStateException | IllegalArgumentException expected) {\n            check(name, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void check(String name, boolean condition) {\n        if (condition) {\n            passed++;\n        } else {\n            failed++;\n            System.out.println(\"FAILED: \" + name);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

// usage: node preview.cjs <puml-name>
// Renders docs/diagrams/src/<name>.puml to SVG with the scratchpad PlantUML jar, then to PNG with Chrome.
const { execFileSync } = require('child_process');
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const SP = __dirname;
const name = process.argv[2];
const src = path.join(process.argv[3] || 'd:/Java Projects/SC2002GroupProject/docs/diagrams/src', name + '.puml');
const out = path.join(SP, 'out');
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
for (const f of fs.readdirSync(out)) if (f.startsWith(name)) fs.rmSync(path.join(out, f));
execFileSync('java', ['-jar', path.join(SP, 'plantuml', 'plantuml-1.2025.4.jar'), '-tsvg', '-charset', 'UTF-8', '-o', out, src], { stdio: 'inherit' });
const files = fs.readdirSync(out).filter((f) => new RegExp(`^${name}(_\\d{3})?\\.svg$`).test(f)).sort();
const chrome = 'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe';
for (const f of files) {
  const svg = fs.readFileSync(path.join(out, f), 'utf8');
  const [, , w, h] = svg.match(/viewBox="([\d.\s]+)"/)[1].trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
  const png = path.join(out, f.replace('.svg', '.png'));
  const html = path.join(out, f.replace('.svg', '.html'));
  fs.writeFileSync(html, `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><style>html,body{margin:0;background:#fff}svg{display:block}</style>${svg.replace(/^<\?xml[^>]*>/, '')}`);
  execFileSync(chrome, ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars', `--window-size=${Math.ceil(w)},${Math.ceil(h)}`, `--screenshot=${png}`, 'file:///' + html.replace(/\\/g, '/')], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  console.log(png.replace(/\\/g, '/'), Math.ceil(w) + 'x' + Math.ceil(h));
}
----
const http=require('http'),fs=require('fs'),path=require('path');
const root=path.resolve(process.argv[2]);
http.createServer((req,res)=>{
  const u=decodeURIComponent(req.url.split('?')[0]);
  const fp=path.join(root,u==='/'?'index.html':u);
  if(!fp.startsWith(root)||!fs.existsSync(fp)){res.writeHead(404);return res.end('nf')}
  const ext=path.extname(fp);
  res.writeHead(200,{'Content-Type':ext==='.html'?'text/html':ext==='.pdf'?'application/pdf':'application/octet-stream'});
  fs.createReadStream(fp).pipe(res);
}).listen(48213,'127.0.0.1',()=>console.log('up'));

// usage: node crop.cjs <svg-name-in-out> x y w h scale  -> out/<name>-crop.png (x,y,w,h in SVG px)
const { execFileSync } = require('child_process');
const fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');

const svg = fs.readFileSync(path.join(out, name + '.svg'), 'utf8').replace(/^<\?xml[^>]*>/, '');
const html = path.join(out, name + '-crop.html');
fs.writeFileSync(html, `<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><style>html,body{margin:0;background:#fff}#v{width:${w * s}px;height:${h * s}px;overflow:hidden;position:relative}#i{position:absolute;left:${-x * s}px;top:${-y * s}px;transform-origin:0 0;transform:scale(${s})}</style><div id="v"><div id="i">${svg}</div></div>`);
const png = path.join(out, name + '-crop.png');
execFileSync('C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--hide-scrollbars', `--window-size=${Math.ceil(w * s)},${Math.ceil(h * s)}`, `--screenshot=${png}`, 'file:///' + html.split(path.sep).join('/')], { stdio: 'ignore' });
console.log(png.split(path.sep).join('/'));

~~~json
{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\README.md",
  "content": "# SC2002 Restaurant Rush\n\nA turn-based restaurant management game for the command line, written in Java for the SC2002 group project.\nThe player is the manager: staff time is limited, so the manager decides which customer or order comes first.\n\nRepository: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject\n\n## Build and run\n\nNeeds JDK 17 or newer. No other dependencies, no network. Run every command from the repository root.\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main\n```\n\nReplay a recorded run instead of typing the choices (the file is read and echoed, so the screen shows a transcript):\n\n```\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt\n```\n\nRun the automated checks (see [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md) for what they cover and for the expected results of both runs):\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests\n```\n\n## Stage tags\n\nEach stage is a separate tagged commit that builds and runs on its own. `runs/` and the tests in each tag belong to that stage.\n\n| Tag | Adds |\n|---|---|\n| `stage-1` | Basic operation: Regular customers, menu, Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Table, Order, the waiting queue, the full six-step turn, abandonment, victory and defeat |\n| `stage-2` | Customer behaviour: VIP and Critic customers next to the Regulars |\n\n## How to play\n\nThe base setting is fixed: 18 turns, 2 tables, one Waiter, one Chef, one Cashier, and one new customer at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16.\n\n| Item | Price | Preparation units |\n|---|---|---|\n| Salad | $9.00 | 1 |\n| Burger | $15.00 | 1 |\n| Pasta | $18.00 | 2 |\n\n| Customer | Arrives | Loses per waiting turn | Bill | Special behaviour |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Regular | turns 4, 7, 13, 16 (customers 2, 3, 5, 6) | 8 | full price | none |\n| VIP | turn 1 (customer 1) | 5 | 10% off | none |\n| Critic | turn 10 (customer 4) | 12 | full price | always orders the most expensive dish (Pasta); loses a further 10 satisfaction if served more than 2 turns after the order was taken |\n\nEvery turn the screen shows the turn number, revenue, the average satisfaction of paid customers, the waiting queue, the tables, every customer's type, satisfaction and order status, and the tasks the staff can do. Then you decide:\n\n| Prompt | Commands | What happens |\n|---|---|---|\n| Waiter (one task per turn) | `SEAT <#>`, `TAKE_ORDER <#>`, `SERVE <#>`, `WAIT` | seat a queued customer at a free table, take a seated customer's order, or serve a READY order |\n| Chef (one unit per turn) | `COOK <#>`, `WAIT` | advance a customer's order by one preparation unit |\n\n`<#>` is the customer number shown on screen (`#2` and `2` both work). It may be left out when only one customer qualifies. A blank line means `WAIT`. A choice that cannot be done is explained and you choose again; it does not use up the staff task.\n\nAfter your choices the turn finishes on its own: customers who were served earlier finish eating (READY_TO_PAY), the Cashier charges at most one customer who became READY_TO_PAY on an earlier turn, and every customer who is still waiting for service loses satisfaction (the amount depends on the customer type). A customer who reaches 0 leaves without paying, the order is cancelled and the table is freed.\n\nAfter turn 18 you win if at least 4 customers have paid, revenue is at least $45.00 and the average satisfaction of the customers who paid is at least 60. Otherwise you lose. The game never ends early.\n\nRules this implementation fixes where the brief leaves a choice:\n\n- **Food choice.** Regular and VIP customers take menu entry number `customerId mod 3` (Salad is 0, Burger 1, Pasta 2), so customer 1 orders a Burger, 2 a Pasta, 3 a Salad, 4 a Burger, and so on. The Critic ignores that rule and takes the most expensive dish. The manager never picks the food; the choice is shown when the order is taken.\n- **Price lock.** The bill is worked out when the Waiter takes the order (a VIP gets 10% off, rounded to the cent) and is stored in the Order; the Cashier collects that amount.\n- **Critic reaction.** \"Served more than 2 turns after the order was taken\" means the serving turn minus the order turn is 3 or more. A Pasta ordered on turn *n* is READY at the end of turn *n*+1 at the earliest and served on turn *n*+2, so a prompt Waiter never upsets the Critic.\n- **Order of events in a turn.** Arrival, your Waiter choice, your Chef choice (so the Chef can work on an order taken this turn), eating and payment, then waiting and abandonment. A dish the Chef finishes this turn is READY only after the Waiter has acted, so it is served next turn at the earliest.\n- **Eating and payment.** A customer served on turn *n* is READY_TO_PAY on turn *n*+1 and can pay on turn *n*+2. \"Oldest first\" means the customer who arrived first.\n\n## From stage-1 to stage-2\n\nCompare: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject/compare/stage-1...stage-2\n\nThe Stage 2 commits are `efec627`, `69a34cb`, `bc1cd9f` and `10f4b5e` plus the diagram and documentation commits after them.\n\n**New files**\n\n| File | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `entity/Menu.java` | Owns the dishes and finds the most expensive one (the Critic needs it) |\n| `entity/RegularCustomer.java` | Baseline customer: loses 8, full price |\n| `entity/VIPCustomer.java` | Loses 5, bill is 90% of the list price |\n| `entity/CriticCustomer.java` | Loses 12, orders the most expensive dish, upset by slow service |\n| `control/ArrivalSchedule.java` | Creates the six base-setting arrivals and decides their type (VIP on turn 1, Critic on turn 10) |\n\n**Existing files changed, and why**\n\n| File | Change | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| `entity/Customer.java` | Became abstract; added the hooks `getTypeLabel`, `getSatisfactionLoss`, `calculateBillCents`, `onServed` and `adjustSatisfaction`; `chooseMenuItem` takes a `Menu`; the fixed loss of 8 moved to `RegularCustomer` | Customer types must differ without the controller testing the type. All turn guards (`canFinishEating`, `canPay`) and the status rules are untouched |\n| `entity/Order.java` | Constructor takes the bill and the turn it was taken; `getPriceCents()` became `getBillCents()` | The VIP discount means the price is no longer the item's list price, so the bill is locked in the order when it is taken. The status transitions are untouched |\n| `entity/Waiter.java` | `takeOrder` takes a `Menu` and the turn, asks the customer for the dish and the bill, and returns the `Order`; `serveOrder` calls `customer.onServed` | This is where type-specific behaviour is dispatched: the Waiter calls the methods on `Customer` and never checks the type |\n| `entity/Cashier.java` | Reads `getBillCents()` | Follows the Order change; one line |\n| `entity/Restaurant.java` | Holds a `Menu` instead of a list; `registerArrival(Customer)` receives the customer instead of creating one | The schedule now decides which kind of customer arrives |\n| `control/GameController.java` | Holds an `ArrivalSchedule` and asks it for arrivals; passes the `Menu` and turn to the Waiter; messages show the customer type | Registration of the new classes. The six-step turn, the guards and the result rule are unchanged |\n| `boundary/RestaurantCLI.java` | Shows the type, and the locked bill of each order | Display only |\n| `runs/victory.txt`, `runs/defeat.txt` | Replaced | The base-setting arrivals now include a VIP and a Critic, so the Stage 1 inputs no longer fit. The Stage 1 runs are still in the `stage-1` tag |\n| `test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java` | Extended | See below |\n\n**Unchanged:** `Chef`, `Table`, `MenuItem`, `CustomerStatus`, `OrderStatus`, `Money`, `Main`.\n\n**Deleted:** nothing.\n\n**What still works.** `GameTests` keeps the Stage 1 checks that still apply (menu, order status rules, the Regular's loss of 8, the turn guards for eating and payment, the Table, payment and abandonment), rewritten only where a constructor changed, and adds the Stage 2 checks. It also checks that `GameController` contains no `instanceof` and never names a customer type. See [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md).\n\n## Repository layout\n\n| Path | Contents |\n|---|---|\n| `src/restaurantrush/` | Java source. `entity/` holds the domain classes, `control/` the turn controller and the arrival schedule, `boundary/` the command-line interface |\n| `test/restaurantrush/` | `GameTests`, plain-Java checks |\n| `runs/` | Recorded manager choices that replay a victory and a defeat |\n| `docs/` | Assignment brief and briefing, test notes, UML diagrams (`docs/diagrams`, sources in `docs/diagrams/src`) |\n| `docs/individual/<member>/` | Each member's `reflection/` and `chat-history/` folders |\n\nThe `individual` folders are directories in this repository, not Git branches.\n"
}
~~~

Cashier collects $18.00 from the Critic (satisfaction 30) |\n| 18 | WAIT | WAIT | Cashier collects $18.00 from #5 (satisfaction 76): the fifth payment |\n\nThe choice in turns 13 to 15 changes the outcome: serving the Critic first would keep their satisfaction at 52 but leave the Regular too late to pay by turn 18, giving 4 paid customers instead of 5.\n\n### Reproducible defeat run\n\n```\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt\n```\n\nThe manager serves the VIP through to payment and then only chooses WAIT. A happy VIP is not enough: all three targets must be met.\n\n### Invalid input\n\n`runs/victory.txt` contains a rejected Waiter choice in turn 17 (`SEAT 6` with both tables occupied). 305:  * Waiter takes the order of Customer #6 (Regular). They choose Burger (list price $15.00, 1 preparation unit(s)). Bill locked at $15.00.

check(\"victory run: average 81.2\", transcript.contains(\"Average satisfaction of paid customers: 81.2\"));\n        check(\"the game ran all 18 turns\", transcript.contains(\"Turns used: 18\"));\n        check(\"exactly six customers arrive in the base setting\", transcript.contains(\"  #6 \") && !transcript.contains(\"  #7 \"));\n        check(\"Happy Hour is offered only until it is used\", count(transcript, \"Happy Hour [y\") == 1);\n        check(\"the status shows Happy Hour's remaining duration\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Happy Hour: ACTIVE, 1 turn(s) left including this one\"));\n        check(\"the status shows Happy Hour as used afterwards\", transcript.contains(\"Happy Hour: used\"));\n        check(\"the VIP's Burger is locked at $10.80 during Happy Hour\",\n                transcript.contains(\"They choose Burger (list price $15.00, 1 preparation unit(s)). Bill locked at $10.80.\"));\n        check(\"the VIP pays $10.80\", transcript.contains(\"Cashier collects $10.80 from Customer #1 (VIP)\"));\n        check(\"a Regular orders the Salad + Burger combo at $21.60, 2 units\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Customer #3 (Regular). They choose Salad + Burger (list price $21.60, 2 preparation unit(s))\"));\n        check(\"the Critic orders the Salad + Pasta combo at $24.30, 3 units\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Customer #4 (Critic). They choose Salad + Pasta (list price $24.30, 3 preparation unit(s))\"));\n        check(\"the Critic, served 3 turns after ordering, pays with satisfaction 54\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Cashier collects $24.30 from Customer #4 (Critic) (satisfaction 54)\"));\n        check(\"the Host seats a customer and the Waiter takes the order in the same turn\", transcript.contains(\n                \"* Host seats Customer #1 (VIP) at Table 1.\\n\"\n                        + \"Waiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: TAKE_ORDER 1\\n\"\n                        + \"  * Waiter takes the order of Customer #1 (VIP).\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void defeatRunIsReproducible() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = playFile(\"runs/defeat.txt\");\n        check(\"defeat run ends in DEFEAT\", transcript.contains(\"\\nDEFEAT\\n\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: only the VIP paid\", transcript.contains(\"Paid customers: 1 (need 4)\")\n                && transcript.contains(\"Revenue: $10.80 (need\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: average 100.0\", transcript.contains(\"Average satisfaction of paid customers: 100.0 (need\"));\n        check(\"defeat run: a Regular and the Critic walked out\", transcript.contains(\"Unhappy departures: 2\")\n                && transcript.contains(\"Customer #2 (Regular) runs out of patience\")\n                && transcript.contains(\"Customer #4 (Critic) runs out of patience\"));\n    }\n\n    private static void invalidChoicesDoNotUseUpTheTask() throws IOException {\n        String transcript = playFile(\"runs/victory.txt\");\n        check(\"a Host request for a customer who has not arrived is explained\", transcript.contains(\"There is no customer #5\"));\n        check(\"a Waiter request for a customer who has not arrived is explained\", transcript.contains(\"There is no customer #3. Choose again.\"));\n        check(\"a rejected Host choice is followed by another Host prompt in the same turn\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Choose again.\\nHost    [SEAT <#> | WAIT]: \"));\n        check(\"a rejected Waiter choice is followed by another Waiter prompt in the same turn\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Choose again.\\nWaiter  [SEAT <#> | TAKE_ORDER <#> | SERVE <#> | WAIT]: \"));\n    }\n\n    private static void happyHourIsOfferedAgainAfterBeingDeclined() {\n        // Turn 1: decline Happy Hour, then WAIT for the Host, Waiter and Chef; turn 2: nonsense, then accept.\n        String transcript = playText(\"n\\nWAIT\\nWAIT\\nWAIT\\nmaybe\\ny\\n\");\n        check(\"Happy Hour is offered again on the next turn\", count(transcript, \"Happy Hour [y\") == 3);\n        check(\"an answer other than y or n is rejected\", transcript.contains(\"'maybe' is not y or n\"));\n        check(\"accepting it on turn 2 switches it on for that turn and the next\",\n                transcript.contains(\"Happy Hour is ON: orders taken now get a discount and waiting customers recover \"\n                        + \"satisfaction. Turns left: 2.\"));\n    }\n\n    // ---- tiny helpers --------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    private static Menu baseMenu() {\n        return new Menu(List.of(SALAD, BURGER, PASTA));\n    }\n\n    private static Restaurant restaurantWithHappyHourOn() {\n        Restaurant restaurant = new Restaurant();\n        restaurant.getHappyHour().activate();\n        return restaurant;\n    }\n\n    /** Seats the customer and has the Waiter take their order at turn 2. */\n    private static Order orderFor(Restaurant restaurant, Customer customer) {\n        restaurant.registerArrival(customer);\n        restaurant.getWaiter().seatCustomer(customer, restaurant.findAvailableTable());\n        restaurant.getWaitingQueue().remove(customer);\n        return restaurant.getWaiter().takeOrder(customer, restaurant.getMenu(), restaurant.getHappyHour(), 2);\n    }\n\n    private static String playFile(String inputFile) throws IOException {\n        try (FileInputStream choices = new FileInputStream(inputFile)) {\n            return play(choices);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static String playText(String choices) {\n        return play(new ByteArrayInputStream(choices.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));\n    }\n\n    private static String play(InputStream choices) {\n        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        try (PrintStream out = new PrintStream(captured, true, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {\n            new RestaurantCLI(choices, out, true).start();\n        }\n        return captured.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).replace(\"\\r\\n\", \"\\n\");\n    }\n\n    private static int count(String text, String part) {\n        int found = 0;\n        for (int at = text.indexOf(part); at >= 0; at = text.indexOf(part, at + 1)) {\n            found++;\n        }\n        return found;\n    }\n\n    private static void expectRejected(String name, Runnable action) {\n        try {\n            action.run();\n            check(name, false);\n        } catch (IllegalStateException | IllegalArgumentException expected) {\n            check(name, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static void check(String name, boolean condition) {\n        if (condition) {\n            passed++;\n        } else {\n            failed++;\n            System.out.println(\"FAILED: \" + name);\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
}

### Tool call: Write (toolu_01MW7XFH11DvfJFM74X4mZ1s)

~~~json
{
  "file_path": "d:\\Java Projects\\SC2002GroupProject\\README.md",
  "content": "# SC2002 Restaurant Rush\n\nA turn-based restaurant management game for the command line, written in Java for the SC2002 group project.\nThe player is the manager: staff time is limited, so the manager decides which customer or order comes first.\n\nRepository: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject\n\n## Build and run\n\nNeeds JDK 17 or newer. No other dependencies, no network. Run every command from the repository root.\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main\n```\n\nReplay a recorded run instead of typing the choices (the file is read and echoed, so the screen shows a transcript):\n\n```\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/victory.txt\njava -cp out restaurantrush.Main --input runs/defeat.txt\n```\n\nRun the automated checks (see [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md) for what they cover and for the expected results of both runs):\n\n```\njavac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java\njava -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests\n```\n\n## Stage tags\n\nEach stage is a separate tagged commit that builds and runs on its own. `runs/` and the tests in each tag belong to that stage, so the recorded runs of an earlier stage are in its tag (`git checkout stage-1`).\n\n| Tag | Adds |\n|---|---|\n| `stage-1` | Basic operation: Regular customers, menu, Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Table, Order, the waiting queue, the full six-step turn, abandonment, victory and defeat |\n| `stage-2` | Customer behaviour: VIP and Critic customers next to the Regulars |\n| `stage-3` | New role and menu policy: the Host, ComboMeal and Happy Hour |\n\n## How to play\n\nThe base setting is fixed: 18 turns, 2 tables, one Waiter, one Chef, one Cashier, one Host (Stage 3), and one new customer at the start of turns 1, 4, 7, 10, 13 and 16.\n\n| Menu entry | Price | Preparation units |\n|---|---|---|\n| Salad | $9.00 | 1 |\n| Burger | $15.00 | 1 |\n| Pasta | $18.00 | 2 |\n| Salad + Burger (combo) | $21.60 | 2 |\n| Salad + Pasta (combo) | $24.30 | 3 |\n\nA combo is made of two existing menu items: its price is 90% of the two prices added together (rounded to the nearest cent) and its preparation units are the sum. Customers pick their own food; the manager never does.\n\n| # | Customer | Arrives | Loses per waiting turn | Bill | Orders |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | VIP | turn 1 | 5 | 10% off | Burger |\n| 2 | Regular | turn 4 | 8 | full price | Pasta |\n| 3 | Regular | turn 7 | 8 | full price | Salad + Burger |\n| 4 | Critic | turn 10 | 12 | full price | Salad + Pasta (the most expensive entry); loses a further 10 satisfaction if served more than 2 turns after the order was taken |\n| 5 | Regular | turn 13 | 8 | full price | Salad |\n| 6 | Regular | turn 16 | 8 | full price | Burger |\n\nEvery turn the screen shows the turn number, revenue, the average satisfaction of paid customers, Happy Hour (and its remaining duration), the waiting queue, the tables, every customer's type, satisfaction and order status, and the tasks each staff member can do. Then you decide:\n\n| Prompt | Commands | What happens |\n|---|---|---|\n| Happy Hour (only while unused) | `y`, `n` (or Enter) | `y` switches Happy Hour on for this turn and the next; it can be used once per game |\n| Host (one task per turn) | `SEAT <#>`, `WAIT` | seat a queued customer at a free table, in addition to the Waiter's task |\n| Waiter (one task per turn) | `SEAT <#>`, `TAKE_ORDER <#>`, `SERVE <#>`, `WAIT` | seat a queued customer, take a seated customer's order, or serve a READY order |\n| Chef (one unit per turn) | `COOK <#>`, `WAIT` | advance a customer's order by one preparation unit |\n\n`<#>` is the customer number shown on screen (`#2` and `2` both work). It may be left out when only one customer qualifies. A blank line means `WAIT`. A choice that cannot be done is explained and you choose again; it does not use up the staff task.\n\nAfter your choices the turn finishes on its own: customers who were served earlier finish eating (READY_TO_PAY), the Cashier charges at most one customer who became READY_TO_PAY on an earlier turn, and every customer who is still waiting for service loses satisfaction (the amount depends on the customer type). A customer who reaches 0 leaves without paying, the order is cancelled and the table is freed.\n\nAfter turn 18 you win if at least 4 customers have paid, revenue is at least $45.00 and the average satisfaction of the customers who paid is at least 60. Otherwise you lose. The game never ends early.\n\n### Happy Hour\n\nSwitched on at the start of a turn, it lasts for that turn and the next, and the screen shows the turns left. While it is on:\n\n- orders taken get 20% off their bill. The VIP discount is applied first, then Happy Hour: a VIP's Burger is $15.00 x 0.9 x 0.8 = $10.80; a Regular's Pasta is $14.40;\n- at the start of each active turn, customers still waiting for a table, to order, or for food recover 15 satisfaction (never above 100).\n\nThe bill is locked when the order is taken, so Happy Hour never changes an order already placed and never touches customers who have left.\n\n### Rules this implementation fixes where the brief leaves a choice\n\n- **Food choice.** Regular and VIP customers take menu entry number `customerId mod 5` (the menu has five entries: Salad 0, Burger 1, Pasta 2, Salad + Burger 3, Salad + Pasta 4), so customer 1 orders a Burger, 2 a Pasta, 3 the Salad + Burger combo, 4 the Salad + Pasta combo, 5 a Salad. The Critic ignores that rule and takes the most expensive entry. The choice is shown when the order is taken.\n- **Price lock.** The bill is worked out when the Waiter takes the order (customer's own pricing first, then Happy Hour) and is stored in the Order; the Cashier collects that amount.\n- **Critic reaction.** \"Served more than 2 turns after the order was taken\" means the serving turn minus the order turn is 3 or more. A 3-unit combo ordered on turn *n* is READY at the end of turn *n*+2 at the earliest and served on turn *n*+3, so the Critic's combo always costs the extra 10 satisfaction; with a 2-unit dish a prompt Waiter avoids it.\n- **Order of events in a turn.** Arrival, Happy Hour choice and recovery, your Host choice, your Waiter choice, your Chef choice (so the Chef can work on an order taken this turn, and the Waiter can take the order of a customer the Host has just seated), eating and payment, then waiting and abandonment. A dish the Chef finishes this turn is READY only after the Waiter has acted, so it is served next turn at the earliest.\n- **Eating and payment.** A customer served on turn *n* is READY_TO_PAY on turn *n*+1 and can pay on turn *n*+2. \"Oldest first\" means the customer who arrived first.\n\n## From stage-1 to stage-2\n\nCompare: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject/compare/stage-1...stage-2\n\nThe Stage 2 commits are `efec627`, `69a34cb`, `bc1cd9f` and `10f4b5e` plus the diagram and documentation commits after them.\n\n**New files**\n\n| File | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `entity/Menu.java` | Owns the dishes and finds the most expensive one (the Critic needs it) |\n| `entity/RegularCustomer.java` | Baseline customer: loses 8, full price |\n| `entity/VIPCustomer.java` | Loses 5, bill is 90% of the list price |\n| `entity/CriticCustomer.java` | Loses 12, orders the most expensive dish, upset by slow service |\n| `control/ArrivalSchedule.java` | Creates the six base-setting arrivals and decides their type (VIP on turn 1, Critic on turn 10) |\n\n**Existing files changed, and why**\n\n| File | Change | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| `entity/Customer.java` | Became abstract; added the hooks `getTypeLabel`, `getSatisfactionLoss`, `calculateBillCents`, `onServed` and `adjustSatisfaction`; `chooseMenuItem` takes a `Menu`; the fixed loss of 8 moved to `RegularCustomer` | Customer types must differ without the controller testing the type. All turn guards (`canFinishEating`, `canPay`) and the status rules are untouched |\n| `entity/Order.java` | Constructor takes the bill and the turn it was taken; `getPriceCents()` became `getBillCents()` | The VIP discount means the price is no longer the item's list price, so the bill is locked in the order when it is taken. The status transitions are untouched |\n| `entity/Waiter.java` | `takeOrder` takes a `Menu` and the turn, asks the customer for the dish and the bill, and returns the `Order`; `serveOrder` calls `customer.onServed` | This is where type-specific behaviour is dispatched: the Waiter calls the methods on `Customer` and never checks the type |\n| `entity/Cashier.java` | Reads `getBillCents()` | Follows the Order change; one line |\n| `entity/Restaurant.java` | Holds a `Menu` instead of a list; `registerArrival(Customer)` receives the customer instead of creating one | The schedule now decides which kind of customer arrives |\n| `control/GameController.java` | Holds an `ArrivalSchedule` and asks it for arrivals; passes the `Menu` and turn to the Waiter; messages show the customer type | Registration of the new classes. The six-step turn, the guards and the result rule are unchanged |\n| `boundary/RestaurantCLI.java` | Shows the type, and the locked bill of each order | Display only |\n| `runs/victory.txt`, `runs/defeat.txt` | Replaced | The base-setting arrivals now include a VIP and a Critic, so the Stage 1 inputs no longer fit. The Stage 1 runs are still in the `stage-1` tag |\n| `test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java` | Extended | See below |\n\n**Unchanged:** `Chef`, `Table`, `MenuItem`, `CustomerStatus`, `OrderStatus`, `Money`, `Main`.\n\n**Deleted:** nothing.\n\n**What still works.** `GameTests` keeps the Stage 1 checks that still apply (menu, order status rules, the Regular's loss of 8, the turn guards for eating and payment, the Table, payment and abandonment), rewritten only where a constructor changed, and adds the Stage 2 checks. It also checks that `GameController` contains no `instanceof` and never names a customer type.\n\n## From stage-2 to stage-3\n\nCompare: https://github.com/RobotHanzo/SC2002GroupProject/compare/stage-2...stage-3\n\nThe Stage 3 commits are `d6fbec7`, `0231d21`, `9ed8d12`, `9225d1b`, `7d210d3` and `a6e64d3` plus the diagram and documentation commits after them.\n\n**New files**\n\n| File | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `entity/ComboMeal.java` | A menu item made of two existing menu items (composition); computes the 90% price and the summed preparation units |\n| `entity/HappyHour.java` | The pricing policy: once per game, two turns, 20% off new orders, +15 satisfaction for waiting customers |\n| `entity/Host.java` | The new staff role: seats one queued customer per turn on top of the Waiter's task |\n\n**Existing files changed, and why**\n\n| File | Change | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n| `entity/MenuItem.java` | No longer `final` (one word) | `ComboMeal` extends it, so Waiter, Chef, Cashier, Menu and Order treat a combo like any dish and need no change |\n| `entity/Customer.java` | Added `recoverSatisfaction`; `isAwaitingService()` became public | Happy Hour recovery and the controller select customers who are still waiting. The existing satisfaction clamp (`adjustSatisfaction`) is reused |\n| `entity/Waiter.java` | `takeOrder` also takes the `HappyHour` and applies it after the customer's own price | Happy Hour is applied after the VIP discount and locked in the Order. Seating, serving and the Critic hook are untouched |\n| `entity/Restaurant.java` | Owns a `Host` and a `HappyHour` (with getters); the menu now lists two combos | Registration of the new classes and of the combos, once, at set-up. Queue, table, payment and abandonment logic is untouched |\n| `control/GameController.java` | New steps before the staff choices: Happy Hour activation and recovery, and the Host choice; `HappyHour.endTurn()` at the end of the turn; the waiting loop uses `isAwaitingService()` | The Stage 3 turn needs these steps. The Waiter and Chef logic, payment and result rule are untouched |\n| `boundary/RestaurantCLI.java` | Host and Happy Hour prompts, Happy Hour status line, Host task line | Display and input only |\n| `runs/victory.txt`, `runs/defeat.txt` | Replaced | There are two more prompts (Happy Hour and Host), and the menu changes what customers order. The Stage 2 runs are in the `stage-2` tag |\n| `test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java` | Extended | See below |\n\n**Unchanged:** `Menu`, `Order`, `Table`, `Chef`, `Cashier`, `ArrivalSchedule`, `RegularCustomer`, `VIPCustomer`, `CriticCustomer`, the enums, `Money`, `Main`.\n\n**Deleted:** nothing.\n\n**What still works.** `GameTests` keeps every Stage 1 and Stage 2 check (the Stage 1 checks that used the three-dish menu now build that menu explicitly, because the game menu has five entries) and adds the combo reference values, Happy Hour timing, discount, price lock and recovery, and the Host. The recorded runs replay the whole game. See [docs/test-notes.md](docs/test-notes.md).\n\n## Design evidence\n\n| Item | Location |\n|---|---|\n| Stage 1, 2 and 3 class diagrams | `docs/diagrams/stage-1-class.pdf`, `stage-2-class.pdf`, `stage-3-class.pdf` |\n| Stage 3 sequence diagram (a VIP orders a Burger with Happy Hour on, turns 1 to 5) | `docs/diagrams/stage-3-sequence.pdf` |\n| Diagram sources | `docs/diagrams/src/*.puml` |\n\n## Video timestamps\n\nTo be filled in by the group after the video is recorded.\n\n| # | Segment | Time |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | The game launching from the submitted project | |\n| 2 | Meaningful manager choices, including a decision where staff are constrained | |\n| 3 | A dish going READY, SERVED, then payment | |\n| 4 | A VIP or Critic behaviour | |\n| 5 | A Stage 3 feature | |\n| 6 | One victory and one defeat | |\n| 7 | The stage tag comparisons and the diagrams | |\n\n## AI history\n\nEach member's AI chats are in their own folder: [Li MU-EN](docs/individual/Li%20MU-EN/chat-history), [Peramudhan](docs/individual/Peramudhan/chat-history), [Ryan](docs/individual/Ryan/chat-history), [Ye Fei](docs/individual/Ye%20Fei/chat-history).\n\n## Repository layout\n\n| Path | Contents |\n|---|---|\n| `src/restaurantrush/` | Java source. `entity/` holds the domain classes, `control/` the turn controller and the arrival schedule, `boundary/` the command-line interface |\n| `test/restaurantrush/` | `GameTests`, plain-Java checks |\n| `runs/` | Recorded manager choices that replay a victory and a defeat |\n| `docs/` | Assignment brief and briefing, test notes, UML diagrams (`docs/diagrams`, sources in `docs/diagrams/src`) |\n| `docs/individual/<member>/` | Each member's `reflection/` and `chat-history/` folders |\n\nThe `individual` folders are directories in this repository, not Git branches.\n"
}
~~~

## 0590 Assistant — 2026-11-02T19:16:06.120Z

Source record: 1713; UUID: 46e078fb-5b93-482e-a9f7-e6dc575c48d0.

I wrote the game in Java across three stages, committed it step by step, and tagged `stage-1`, `stage-2` and `stage-3`. Each tag builds and passes its own tests from a clean `git archive` export. Both recorded runs give the expected result at each tag. Nothing is pushed: `main` is 20 commits ahead of `origin/main` and the tags are local only.

| Tag | Tests | Victory run | Defeat run |
|---|---|---|---|
| `stage-1` (`870cfb1`) | 55 pass | 4 paid, $57.00, average 78.0 | 0 paid |
| `stage-2` (`6114930`) | 108 pass | 5 paid, $76.50, average 69.6 | 1 paid |
| `stage-3` (`aec304a`) | 164 pass | 5 paid, $83.70, average 81.2 | 1 paid |

The Stage 1 and 2 tags contain none of the later classes (Host, HappyHour, ComboMeal; and no VIP or Critic classes in Stage 1).

**How to run**
- Build with `javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src src/restaurantrush/Main.java`, then `java -cp out restaurantrush.Main`.
- Replay a recorded run with `--input runs/victory.txt` or `--input runs/defeat.txt`.
- Run the tests with `javac --release 17 -d out -sourcepath src test/restaurantrush/GameTests.java` followed by `java -cp out restaurantrush.GameTests`.

**Where I departed from the diagrams**
- **Guards stay on `Customer` and `Restaurant`.** The Stage 2 and 3 diagrams listed `canFinishEating`, `canPay` and `findOldestEligiblePayer` as removed and moved into the controller. I kept them where they were, because moving them is an unjustified rewrite of stable logic and the rubric penalises that. The Stage 2 and 3 class diagrams and the sequence diagram now show them.
- **Smaller signature changes while coding.** These were `serveOrder` taking the turn, `recordPayment` taking the amount, `registerArrival` returning the new `Customer`, the CLI getting `start()` and `displayMessage()`, and a served-customer count. I re-rendered all four PDFs to match.
- **Diagram check.** Every member in each class diagram exists in the code with the same visibility, checked against the compiled classes of each tag.

**Rules I had to choose**
- **Arrivals.** Customer 1 (turn 1) is a VIP and customer 4 (turn 10) is a Critic. The rest are Regulars.
- **Food.** Regulars and VIPs take menu entry `customerId mod 5`, so customer 3 gets the Salad + Burger combo. The Critic takes the most expensive entry, the Salad + Pasta combo.
- **Critic penalty.** A 3-unit combo can never be served within 2 turns of ordering, so the Critic's combo always costs them the extra 10 satisfaction. The README and the class diagram say so.

**Things to check or finish**
- **Intermediate commits.** Within Stage 2 and Stage 3, a few intermediate commits fail the recorded-run checks. The prompts or the schedule change before the new run files arrive. Only the tagged commits are verified end to end.
- **Video timestamps.** The README table is empty because the video doesn't exist yet.
- **Compare links.** The `stage-1...stage-2` and `stage-2...stage-3` links in the README only work after you push the tags (`git push origin main --tags`). I didn't push because you only asked for commits and tags.
- **Local README and render script.** `docs/diagrams/src/README.md` and `render.mjs` are still on disk and git-ignored. I refreshed the README's page sizes and alignment notes.
- **Rubric PDF.** It is still untracked and not in any commit.
